### 2.7 notes

- **Spec.** Written first, on 2026-10-18, with every open question settled by its recommended option (the user wants Phase 2 built autonomously): [Timesheet approval, the DTR and the offset balance (build step 2.7)](modules/talent.md#timesheet-approval-the-dtr-and-the-offset-balance-build-step-27) and BUILD_PLAN decisions 277–298. The unconfirmed choices are assumptions 123–137 below; the build added 138–149. Built and verified on 2026-10-18 (see [2.7 review](#27-review-2026-10-18)).
- **Done when:** on a phone, a returned Standard timesheet is resubmitted, approved by a supervisor and HR, locked, and its offset hours credited. Check it with a Standard employee whose cut-off has ended (set `?cutOff=` to a past cut-off, or a test clock): submit, return as the supervisor on `/approvals`, fix an entry and resubmit, approve as the supervisor, then as HR; the timesheet reads "Approved · Locked" and the Offset card "Credited +X h".
- **Settled while writing the spec** (worth the user's eye):
  - **The DTR stays derived, never stored** (decision 288). Step 2.4 decided hours are derived when read, and the "Derived values" table says the same, while the step text says "recompute periods not yet finalized". Both hold: every non-finalized period is always current, and the holidays-changed listener recomputes the one stored value derived from the DTR, the offset credits (decision 292). Step 2.9's payroll run stores the figures it paid.
  - **The deadline needs no new seeded version** (decision 279). Decision 216 said step 2.7 "adds the submission deadline in a later version", but `pnpm seed:admin` never adds to a setting that already has a version, so the field reads 1 when absent.
  - **Late timesheets are accepted** (decision 280). The spec's "after the cut-off ends" with a 1-day deadline leaves a one-day window; refusing late ones would lose overtime and offset.
- **What it builds on.**
  - Step 2.4: `packages/talent/src/timesheets/` (`computeDay`, `computeTimesheet`, `scheduleOn`, `versionOn`; `TIMESHEET_STATUSES` is `['open']` in `schemas.ts`), `server/timesheets/` (`model.ts` with the write guard and the touch, `service.ts` with `TIMESHEET_NOT_OPEN` and `buildView`, `periods.ts`, `open-job.ts`), and `canViewTimesheet` in `server/policy.ts`.
  - Step 2.5: `server/reimbursements/service.ts` (its open check) and `server/receipt-claims/`.
  - Step 2.6: `ONBOARDING_GATES.fileTimeRequest` and `assertOnboardingGate` (`packages/talent/src/onboarding/gates.ts`), the own status in `server/onboarding/service.ts`, and `registerTalentApprovalTypes()` in `server/change-requests/service.ts`, which `apps/web/lib/approval-types.ts` already calls.
  - Core: the approvals engine (`registerApprovalType` with `onStepApproved`, `submitApproval`, `isApproverFor`) in `packages/core/src/server/approvals/`, `registerHolidaysChangedListener` in `packages/core/src/server/holidays/events.ts` (the worker's `core.holidaysChanged` handler already runs the listeners), `notify`, `activeHrUserIds`, `listConfirmedHolidays`. **No Core change is planned.**
  - Step 2.2's expiry alert job (`server/expiry-alerts/`, `scripts/dev-expiry-alerts.ts`) is the model for the reminder job and its dev script.
- **Build order (planned).** Each chunk owns the files listed; nothing outside its list. Chunks on the same line run in parallel and touch no common file.
  1. **In parallel:**
     - **A. DTR pure rules.** Owns the new `packages/talent/src/dtr/` only: `DTR_DAY_STATUSES` and labels, `computeDtr` (day statuses, late, undertime, night differential, overtime or offset, `final`, totals, with a `timeOff` input left empty until step 2.8), its view types, its `index.ts`, and `dtr.test.ts` with cases D1–D12. It reads `timesheets/compute.ts` and `schedule.ts` but doesn't edit them; anything more it needs from the entries it works out itself.
     - **B. Statuses, deadline and offset pure rules, and the setting.** Owns `packages/talent/src/timesheets/schemas.ts` (`TIMESHEET_STATUSES`, labels, editable statuses, `submissionDeadlineDays` defaulting to 1), a new `timesheets/submission.ts` (the deadline, the opening day, "Submitted late", the allowed moves, the reminder days) with its test, `timesheets/views.ts` (the banner, submit summary, list row and DTR segment views) and `timesheets/index.ts`, the new `packages/talent/src/offset/` (`OFFSET_ENTRY_KINDS`, the balance sum, the reconcile difference, views, tests), `packages/talent/src/index.ts` (its exports, and `export * from './dtr'` for chunk A), and `server/payroll-settings/seed-data.ts` with the payroll settings tests that check the `talent.timesheetSettings` value.

     - `server/reimbursements/service.ts` (open or returned), `server/change-requests/service.ts` (only to register `talent.timesheet` in `registerTalentApprovalTypes()`), `server/policy.ts` (the approver view rule), `server/index.ts`, `server/testing/fixtures.ts`, and the tests, the Done when and D13–D14 included

Worker and dev script.** Owns `apps/worker/src/talent-jobs.ts` and its test (the reminder job and schedule, and the listener registered at start-up, or in `apps/worker/src/main.ts` if it must run before the handlers), a new `scripts/dev-timesheet-reminders.ts`, and its line in the root `package.json`.

### 2.7 review (2026-10-18)

Built in the planned chunks (A and B in parallel, then C, then D, E and F in parallel), then verified and reviewed by separate agents. Typecheck, lint, Prettier, `pnpm test` (88 files, 1499 tests), the production build (`next build`), `pnpm seed:admin` run twice (the second run adds nothing, and 2.7 adds nothing new to the seed) and `pnpm dev:timesheet-reminders` pass. Worth knowing:

- **An `overdue` view flag.** My team's status and the timesheets list rows carry `overdue` (not submitted and past the deadline), set by the new pure helper `isTimesheetOverdue()` in `packages/talent/src/timesheets/submission.ts`, so the "Overdue" badge and row take the destructive tone from one rule (item 148).
- **Listeners at worker start-up.** `registerTalentListeners()` in `apps/worker/src/talent-jobs.ts` registers Talent's holidays-changed listener (`talent.recomputeDtr`), and `apps/worker/src/main.ts` calls it before the job handlers start, so a `core.holidaysChanged` job always finds it. It has its own test.
- **The reconcile fix.** `reconcileTimesheet` in `server/offset/service.ts` touched the approved timesheet (`updatedBy: null`, `__v` raised) before checking anything, even when it then wrote no adjustment. The touch now runs only when an adjustment is written, still in the same transaction, so a concurrent run conflicts, retries and re-reads the entries; the reconcile stays idempotent and the D14 tests pass.
- **A "Timesheets" button, not tiles.** The `/talent` header has a "Timesheets" button to `/talent/timesheets` (decision 294), rather than status tiles on the employees page.
- **No Core change.** Approver access falls back to a Talent service reader (item 142); the approvals engine, the holidays-changed listeners and the notifications are used as they are.
- **New code.** `packages/talent/src/dtr/`, `packages/talent/src/offset/`, `timesheets/submission.ts`, `server/dtr/`, `server/offset/` (the `offsetEntries` collection), `server/timesheets/` (`access.ts`, `approval.ts`, `list.ts`, `reminders.ts`, `status.ts`, `submission.ts`), `scripts/dev-timesheet-reminders.ts`, and the page `/talent/timesheets`.
- **Choices made during the build** are assumptions 138–149 in [Assumptions to check](#assumptions-to-check).

Not checked: the screens in a browser (no browser tools here).

### 2.7 hands-on checks (by the user, signed in)

1. **On a phone, in light and dark (about 390 px):** the status banner, the Submit and Resubmit sheet with its problem links (each opens the day, the reimbursement line or `/talent/onboarding`), the DTR segment with the sticky-footer totals, and the Offset card's "Credited +X h" fit with no sideways scroll.
2. **My team** shows each person's status badge; **`/talent/timesheets`** (from the "Timesheets" button on `/talent`) has the cut-off picker and the status filters, and a not-submitted timesheet past its deadline shows a red "Overdue" row.
3. **Approvals.** As an approver, "Open in Approvals" from the timesheet; approve as the supervisor, then as HR; on another timesheet, try a return with remarks.
4. **The "Done when"**, live on a phone: a returned Standard timesheet is resubmitted, approved by the supervisor and by HR, reads "Approved · Locked", and the Offset card shows its offset hours credited.
5. **The deadline field** in Payroll settings › Work schedule: add a version with a new deadline; a blank field is refused.
6. **The reminders.** Seed a few open timesheets for an ended cut-off (the development database had none), then run `pnpm dev:timesheet-reminders --date <deadline>` and check the employees' and HR's notifications.
7. **A holiday change on an approved period.** Confirm or change a holiday inside an approved, credited timesheet's period: the worker runs the listener, an adjustment is written, and the employee gets `talent.offsetAdjusted`.

### 2.8 notes

- **Spec.** Written first, on 2026-10-18, with every open question settled by its recommended option (the user wants Phase 2 built autonomously): [Leave, offset time off and opening balances (build step 2.8)](modules/talent.md#leave-offset-time-off-and-opening-balances-build-step-28), BUILD_PLAN decisions 299–324, and [Leave and time off](COMPLIANCE.md#leave-and-time-off-build-step-28). The unconfirmed choices, the statutory leave figures among them, are assumptions 150–180 below; the build added 181–221 and verification 222–224. Built and verified on 2026-10-19 (see [2.8 build](#28-build-2026-10-19)) and committed as `d05d7aa`, not pushed.
- **Done when:** on a phone, VL filed 10 days ahead is rejected, and approved offset time off shows as offset (paid) and lowers the balance (cases L1 and O5). Check it with a Standard employee who has an offset balance (from an approved timesheet, or an opening entry after setting the go-live date in Payroll settings › Leave): file VL for 10 days ahead and read the refusal; file offset time off for a whole day; approve it as the supervisor on `/approvals`; the request reads "Approved", the timesheet's DTR segment shows "Offset (paid)" on that day, and the Offset card's balance is 8.0 h lower.
- **Settled while writing the spec** (worth the user's eye):
  - **An HR requester's time off goes to the System Administrator** (decision 313), as decision 2 says, although step 2.7 sends HR's own timesheet to the other HR users. Talent passes the step at submit with `whenEmpty: 'systemAdministrator'` for a requester in the HR department, so no Core change is needed.
  - **Service Incentive Leave** (decision 322). The spec's "VL meets SIL, never converted to cash, credited from regularization" conflicts with the implementing rules (unused SIL commutable to cash at year end, due after one year of service, Contractual included). Built as the spec says, listed for counsel (item 152 and [Open questions](COMPLIANCE.md#open-questions)).
  - **Calendar-day leave** (decision 302). The spec says rest days and holidays inside a leave aren't deducted, but the law counts maternity and special leave for women in calendar days, so each type has a `countDays` setting.
  - **Partly covered days on the DTR** (decision 315) refine item 138: a half day or part-day offset leaves the day `present` when worked, and late and undertime count only against the uncovered part.
  - **HR corrections** (decision 307). Step 2.7 had no manual offset correction (item 132); opening entries need one, so HR adds `adjustment` entries with a reason for leave and offset.
  - **VAWC is confidential** (decision 318), routed to HR only and shown as "Leave" to others, for RA 9262's confidentiality. The spec routes all leave through the supervisor.
  - **The "My work" sidebar group** (decision 231) isn't added; "Time off" joins Timesheet in Pulse Core (decision 319).
- **What it builds on.**
  - Step 2.7: `packages/talent/src/dtr/` (`computeDtr`, `DtrTimeOffInput` and the `timeOff` input left empty until now, `DTR_TIME_OFF_STATUSES`, `statusOf`'s holiday and rest day precedence, `lateAndUndertime`), `packages/talent/src/offset/` (`OFFSET_ENTRY_KINDS` with `opening` and `usage` already declared), `server/offset/` (`offsetEntries` with `sourceType` and `sourceId` already on the model, `getOffsetBalance`), `server/dtr/` (`getDtr`, `loadDtrSources`, the `talent.recomputeDtr` listener), and `timesheets/submission.ts` (the deadline helpers SL's filing window reuses).
  - Step 2.6: `ONBOARDING_GATES.fileTimeRequest` and `assertOnboardingGate`; `registerTalentApprovalTypes()` in `server/change-requests/service.ts`, already called by `apps/web/lib/approval-types.ts`; the edit-and-send-again pattern of change requests.
  - Step 2.4: `scheduleOn` and the schedule's `breakStart` and `breakMinutes` for the halves; step 2.2: the file registrations with `auditView`, image re-encoding, and the 201 medical rule; step 2.3: the payroll settings segments and `defineConfigSetting`; step 2.1: `sensitiveField()`, `registerTalentSensitiveReveals()` and the salary rule.
  - Core: the approvals engine with steps passed at submit and per-step `whenEmpty`, `isApproverFor`, `nextDocumentNumber`, `registerFileAccess`, `notify`, `activeHrUserIds`, `listConfirmedHolidays`. **No Core change was needed** (item 199).
- **Build order** (followed as planned, apart from the two notes in [2.8 build](#28-build-2026-10-19): chunk G ran beside D, and F's route constant isn't used in G's files yet). Each chunk owns the files listed; nothing outside its list. Chunks on the same line run in parallel and touch no common file. Pure rules, server, worker and UI stay apart.
  1. **In parallel:**
     - **A. Time off pure rules.** Owns the new `packages/talent/src/time-off/` only: `LEAVE_ENTRY_KINDS`, `TIME_OFF_KINDS`, `TIME_OFF_PORTIONS`, `TIME_OFF_STATUSES` and their labels; the `talent.leaveSettings` schema and types (with `goLiveDate`, `offsetTimeOff` and the type fields); half-day units and formatting; the halves of a day; chargeable days (`workDays` and `calendarDays`); eligibility; the filing window (using the deadline helper in `timesheets/submission.ts`, read only); the per-event and per-year limits; the available balance and the paid and unpaid split; the regularization proration and carry-over; offset part-day validation (minimum and step, starting after filing); the `LV-` and `OFS-` prefixes; a request's per-day coverage (`TimeOffRequestDay`, its own type, not the DTR's); titles, summaries, the confidential label and views; `time-off.test.ts` with the pure parts of L1–L3, L5, L9–L13 and O2–O4. Also `packages/talent/src/offset/` (the available offset balance and the usage views) and `packages/talent/src/index.ts` (`export * from './time-off'`).
     - **B.   4. **In parallel** (after D):

### 2.8 build (2026-10-19)

Built in the planned chunks (A and B in parallel, then C, then D, then E, F and G), by separate agents, then verified by a separate agent on 2026-10-19 (also in [TESTING › Leave and time off tests](TESTING.md#leave-and-time-off-tests)):

- `pnpm typecheck` and `pnpm lint` pass; `prettier --check` passes on the 96 changed and new files.
- `pnpm test` passes: 92 files, 1615 tests (run before the verifier's final edits; the affected suites were re-run after them: 3 files, 121 tests, all pass).
- `pnpm --filter @pulse/web build` passes with no warnings.
- `pnpm seed:admin`: the first run added `talent.leaveSettings` (1); the second added nothing.
- `pnpm dev:leave-credits`, run twice: the development database has no go-live date set, so nothing was written. Credit idempotence on real data is therefore covered only by the unit tests; hands-on check 8 covers it.
- **One defect found and fixed.** A confidential (VAWC) request leaked its approver remarks, its cancel reason and the approval history's remarks to the supervisors and Talent Read viewers (`requestViewOf` and `getTimeOffRequestPage` in `packages/talent/src/server/time-off/service.ts`). They are now hidden like its reason, and the test is extended (item 222).

Worth knowing:

- **Leave days as text.** `days` and `carryOverCapDays` in `talent.leaveSettings` are stored as text (`"5"`, `"2.5"`), since Core's versioned store refuses fractional numbers, and read as whole half days (item 181, [DATA_MODEL › Versioned configuration](DATA_MODEL.md#versioned-configuration)). The setting has its own view and "Add version" in Payroll settings › Leave, and its `goLiveDate` locks once an opening entry exists (item 186).
- **A per-employee filing lock.** Every filing, resend, approval and listener change of an employee's time off raises one document in the new collection `timeOffFilingLocks` first in its transaction, so the overlap check is serialized per employee (item 204, now in [DATA_MODEL › Collection ownership](DATA_MODEL.md#collection-ownership)).
- **The approver check reads the record type.** Core's `isApproverFor` keys approvers by the record, so the request page, the employee's time off page and the attachment check `talent.timeOffRequest`, not the approval types `talent.leave` and `talent.offsetTimeOff`. SECURITY.md's exception now says so (item 200).
- **The approval re-counts the request** (item 202), but Core's "Approved" notification keeps the title written at submit, so a split changed at approval shows only on the request page and the own list. The spec now says so (item 203).
- **Routing without a Core change** (item 199): the steps are passed at submit with per-step `whenEmpty`; a confidential (VAWC) request goes to HR only, and an HR requester's to the System Administrator.
- **Attachments are medical files** (owner type `talent.timeOffRequest`, re-encoded with the `receipt` transform), opened by the employee, HR or the System Administrator with Talent Read and that request's approvers, each open audited as a `reveal` (items 205, 206).
- **Year-to-date pay** (`payrollOpeningEntries`) is encrypted, registered as the reveal `talent.payrollOpeningEntry` in category `salary`, and corrected by a superseding entry (items 189, 194).
- **The credit job** `talent.leaveCredits` (cron `30 0 * * *`) is registered beside the other Talent jobs; `pnpm dev:leave-credits [--date YYYY-MM-DD]` runs it once in development and refuses in production (item 212). The existing holidays-changed listener now also reconciles approved time off (`talent.leaveAdjusted`).
- **Two chunk notes.** Chunk G ran beside D, so the inspector's Time off section shows the latest leave entries, not the latest requests (item 195), and G's files used the literal `/talent/opening-balances` rather than F's `OPENING_BALANCES_HREF` until verification switched them (item 221).
- **New code.** `packages/talent/src/time-off/` (the pure rules), time off in `packages/talent/src/dtr/`, `server/leave/` (the `leaveEntries` collection, balances, crediting and `runLeaveCredits`), `server/opening-balances/` (`payrollOpeningEntries`), `server/time-off/` (`timeOffRequests`, `timeOffFilingLocks`, the checks, approval and reconcile), offset openings, adjustments and usage in `server/offset/`, `scripts/dev-leave-credits.ts`, and the pages `/talent/time-off`, `/talent/time-off/[employeeId]`, `/talent/time-off-requests/[id]` and `/talent/opening-balances`, with the inspector's Time off and year-to-date pay sections, Payroll settings › Leave, the DTR's time off badges and the navigation entries.
- **Choices made during the build** are assumptions 181–221 in [Assumptions to check](#assumptions-to-check), and those found in verification 222–224. Items 152, 206 and 210 are marked **Legal** for counsel.

Not checked: the screens in a browser (no browser automation here); they are in the [hands-on checks](#28-hands-on-checks-by-the-user-signed-in).

### 2.8 hands-on checks (by the user, signed in)

Before these, rerun `pnpm seed:admin` on existing development databases (it adds `talent.leaveSettings`; a second run adds nothing), and set a go-live date in Payroll settings › Leave.

1. **The "Done when"**, live on a phone, on a throwaway database with a go-live date set: with a Standard employee who has an offset balance, file VL for 10 days ahead and read the 14-day refusal (L1); file offset time off for a whole day, approve it as the supervisor on `/approvals`; the request reads "Approved", the timesheet's DTR segment shows "Offset (paid) 8.0 h" on that day, and the Offset card's balance is 8.0 h lower (O5).
2. **Every new screen in light and dark, at about 390 px with no sideways scroll:** `/talent/time-off` (balance cards, request list, request sheet with its live summary), `/talent/time-off/<employeeId>`, `/talent/time-off-requests/<id>`, `/talent/opening-balances` and its sheets, the inspector's Time off and Year-to-date pay sections, Payroll settings › Leave and its "Add version" sheet, the DTR's time off badges ("Offset (paid) 8.0 h"), and My team's "Time off" links. On `/talent/time-off`, also cancel a request and edit and send again a returned one.
3. **The VAWC confidential label.** File VAWC leave as a female employee: it goes to HR only (no supervisor), and the supervisor, Talent Read and the approval title, summary and notifications show only "Leave" or "Leave (confidential)", never the type, reason, attachment, approver remarks (in the approval history too) or cancel reason.
4. **An HR requester's routing.** As an HR employee with an empty `reportingTo`, file leave: it waits for the System Administrator, not the other HR users.
5. **The attachment open audit.** File SL with a medical certificate; open it as the employee, as HR with Talent Read and as the approving supervisor, and see a `reveal` entry for each in `/admin/audit`; Accounting and an unrelated supervisor get not found.
6. **The year-to-date pay reveal audit.** On `/talent/opening-balances`, enter year-to-date pay, then reveal a figure in the inspector; the audit log has the reveal (category `salary`), and the figures stay masked until revealed.
7. **The go-live lock.** After the first opening entry, Payroll settings › Leave refuses a version that changes the go-live date.
8. **`pnpm dev:leave-credits`**, with and without `--date YYYY-MM-DD` (try a January 1 and a Regular employee's regularization day): it prints the date, the go-live date and the counts, a second run writes nothing, and the balances show the credits.

### 2.9 notes

- **Spec.** Written first, on 2026-10-19, with every open question settled by its recommended option (the user wants Phase 2 built autonomously, rates and law included): [Payroll run computation (build step 2.9)](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29), BUILD_PLAN decisions 325–349, and [Payroll computation](COMPLIANCE.md#payroll-computation-build-step-29). The unconfirmed choices, every pay formula and legal reading among them, are assumptions 225–257 below; items 225, 228–230, 234–236, 238, 243, 250 and 254 are marked **Legal** for the accountant and counsel. The build added 258–285 (none marked Legal; 261 names counsel). Built and verified on 2026-10-19 (see [2.9 build](#29-build-2026-10-19)) and committed as `0360b68`, not pushed.
- **Done when:** a draft run's lines for one Overtime and one Standard employee match a hand calculation from the seeded rates (cases P1 and P2: net ₱11,595.20 and ₱11,092.74). Check it on a throwaway database with the go-live date 2026-09-26: two employees with those salaries, timesheets and approvals as in the cases, then "Compute draft run" for Sep 26 – Oct 10 on `/talent/payroll`, "Show amounts", and open each breakdown.
- **Settled while writing the spec** (worth the user's eye):
  - **Settlements by difference** (decision 339). The spec's "settles in the next cut-off once approved" and "late approvals and changes to finalized periods become adjustments" are one mechanism: each line stores the attendance items it paid per period, and a later run pays the period's items now less what finalized runs paid. Basic pay of a finalized period is never recomputed, so back pay stays the manual line the spec's example names (step 2.10).
  - **Only finalized runs count as paid**, so drafts for two cut-offs can both show a settlement. Step 2.10 must finalize runs in cut-off order and recompute a draft before it is submitted.
  - **Contributions across the two cut-offs** (decision 336). The month's compensation isn't known at mid-month, so the mid-month cut-off deducts half on the monthly basic salary and the end-of-month cut-off the real month less that, as step 2.3's split (end of month takes the remainder) implies.
  - **Holidays for monthly-rated employees** (decision 330). The 261 factor pays every day but rest days, so a monthly salary already covers holidays on work days; only a regular holiday that fails the workday-before rule is deducted, and an unworked special day is a setting (`paid` seeded).
  - **The go-live date** (`talent.leaveSettings.goLiveDate`, step 2.8) also starts payroll: the first payroll cut-off is the first to start on or after it.
  - **Amounts behind "Show amounts"** (decision 343): one audit-logged reveal per page view and per breakdown, instead of masking each figure.
  - **Encrypted as one JSON value** (decision 343): each line's breakdown is one `sensitiveField()` string, so a recompute encrypts a handful of values per employee, not one per line.
- **What it builds on.**
  - Step 2.3: `packages/talent/src/payroll/lookups.ts` (`cutOffFor`, `splitContribution`, `sssRowFor`, `philhealthShares`, `pagibigShares`, `semiMonthlyWithholding`, `deMinimisLimitCentavos`, `dailyRateRatio`), `payroll/allowances.ts` (`resolveAllowances`), the payroll settings and `defineConfigSetting`; step 2.1's compensation timeline (`compensation.ts`) and `sensitiveField()`.
  - Steps 2.4, 2.7 and 2.8: `timesheets/compute.ts` (`DayHours`, `OvertimeByKind`, `DAY_KINDS`), `dtr/` (`computeDtr`, `DtrDay`, `DtrTotals` with `absentMinutes`, `leaveWithoutPayDays`), `server/dtr/service.ts` (`loadDtrSources`, `timesheetDtr`; `getDtr` takes an actor, so the run uses an actorless loader), `server/dtr/listener.ts`, `server/reimbursements/`, `server/time-off/` and `server/leave/` (read only), `talent.leaveSettings.goLiveDate`.
  - Core: `allocateCentavos`, `ratio`, `scaleCentavos` and the money helpers, `nextDocumentNumber`, `notify`, `activeHrUserIds`, `recordAudit` (`reveal`), `registerSensitiveReveal`, `listConfirmedHolidays`, the holidays-changed listener registry. **No Core change was needed.**
- **Build order** (followed as planned; `server/dtr/service.ts` wasn't changed, since the run service's batch loaders in `server/payroll-runs/compute.ts` load the sources themselves and reuse its existing `dtrOf`, and the "Payroll" button went in `employees-manager.tsx`, wired from `page.tsx`). Each chunk owns the files listed; nothing outside its list. Chunks on the same line run in parallel and touch no common file. Pure rules, server, worker and UI stay apart.
  1. **In parallel:**
     - **A. The pure pay engine.** Owns the new `packages/talent/src/payroll-run/` only: `PAYROLL_RUN_KINDS`, `PAYROLL_RUN_STATUSES`, `PAYROLL_LINE_KINDS` and their labels; the `PayrollRunPolicy` input type (the shape of `talent.payrollRunSettings` in DATA_MODEL, declared here so A doesn't wait for B); base days and the rates of each day; basic pay and prorating; holidays and the workday-before rule; premiums; night differential; attendance deductions; allowances and de minimis; reimbursements; contributions (mid-month estimate, end-of-month remainder, no mid-month run) through `payroll/lookups.ts` (read only); withholding and minimum wage earners; the provisional set; attendance items per period and the settlement difference; totals, negative net and basic salary earned; the breakdown's JSON shape (version 1) with its parse and serialize; the line grouping and words for the screens; `payroll-run.test.ts` with P1–P12 and H2 from hand-built inputs. Also `packages/talent/src/index.ts` (`export * from './payroll-run'`). It reads `dtr/`, `timesheets/`, `payroll/` and `compensation.ts` without changing them.
     - **B. The run setting, the seed and the models.** Owns a new `packages/talent/src/payroll/run-settings.ts` (the `talent.payrollRunSettings` schema and type) and its export in `packages/talent/src/payroll/index.ts`; `server/payroll-settings/settings.ts`, `seed-data.ts`, `service.ts` and their tests (the setting, its seed from 2000-01-01, its add-version path and view); `server/seed/index.ts`; and a new `server/payroll-runs/model.ts` (`payrollRuns` and `payrollRunEmployees` with their indexes, `sensitiveField()`s, validators and write guards) with `model.test.ts`.
  2. **After B, in parallel:**
     - **C. The run service** (after A and B; one agent, since the work shares `server/policy.ts`, `server/index.ts` and the fixtures). Owns the rest of `server/payroll-runs/`: the actorless input loaders (employees in scope through Core's identity reads, compensation, allowances, the DTR with time off, reimbursements, holidays, settings versions), the reconciliation against finalized lines, the mid-month pairing, create and recompute (one transaction, `version`), the job function (`PAYROLL_DRAFTS_JOB`, `runPayrollDrafts(at)`) and `talent.payrollDraftReady`, access, the list and run views, "Show amounts" and the breakdown with their `reveal` audit, and `payroll-runs.test.ts` (P1–P12 and H2 end to end, with a fixture that writes finalized runs). Also `server/dtr/listener.ts` (the draft recompute), `server/dtr/service.ts` (only to export an actorless DTR loader if none fits), `server/sensitive/reveals.ts` (`talent.payrollRunEmployee`), `server/policy.ts`, `server/index.ts` and `server/testing/fixtures.ts`.
     - **F. The setting on the page** (after B only). Owns `apps/web/app/(pulse)/talent/payroll-settings/` (the "Payroll run" card and its "Add version" sheet in the Pay schedule segment, in a new `payroll-run-settings.tsx` and the segment's wiring) and `apps/web/lib/actions/talent-payroll.ts`.
  3. **In parallel** (after C):
     - **D. Worker and dev script.** Owns `apps/worker/src/talent-jobs.ts` and its test (`talent.payrollDraftRuns` with cron `0 5 * * *`), a new `scripts/dev-payroll-drafts.ts`, and its line in the root `package.json`.
     - **E. The run screens.** Owns the new `apps/web/app/(pulse)/talent/payroll/` (`page.tsx`, `[runId]/page.tsx`, their loading states, and the list, tiles, people list, totals and breakdown sheet components under it), `apps/web/lib/actions/talent-payroll-runs.ts`, `apps/web/lib/navigation.ts` and its test (`/talent/payroll` as "Payroll" and `/talent/payroll/<id>` as "Payroll run" in Pulse Talent, and a `PAYROLL_HREF` constant), and the "Payroll" button in `apps/web/app/(pulse)/talent/employees-manager.tsx` (or `page.tsx`, whichever holds the header buttons).
  4. **Verification:** typecheck, lint, Prettier, `pnpm test`, the production build, `pnpm seed:admin` twice (the first adds `talent.payrollRunSettings`, the second nothing), `pnpm dev:payroll-drafts` with and without `--date`, and a review of: P1 and P2 to the centavo, no amount in any log, audit entry or notification, the reveal written before any amount is sent, the validators refusing plain amounts, recompute under concurrency, a non-draft refused, and phone width in light and dark.
  5. **Docs:** HANDOFF, BUILD_SUMMARY, the step's results in TESTING, CHANGELOG, and AGENTS.md (the `pnpm dev:payroll-drafts` command, `pnpm seed:admin`'s run settings, and the payroll run tests in the `pnpm test` list); then commit, not push.
- **Risks.** The nightly job and each recompute rebuild 24 cut-offs of DTR per employee for the reconciliation; if it is slow, load each source once per employee for the whole window. The listener recomputes drafts inside the holidays-changed job, so a lost job leaves a draft stale until the next night (step 2.10 recomputes before submit). Every pay figure rests on assumptions 225–257; HR and the accountant check them, and every seeded rate, before the first real payroll.
- **Spec:** [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29), [Payroll](modules/talent.md#payroll) ([Rates](modules/talent.md#rates), [Pay computation per cut-off](modules/talent.md#pay-computation-per-cut-off), [Allowances](modules/talent.md#allowances), [Employer contributions & monthly remittances](modules/talent.md#employer-contributions--monthly-remittances)), [Submission & approval](modules/talent.md#submission--approval), [Daily Time Record (DTR)](modules/talent.md#daily-time-record-dtr), [Payroll settings and allowances](modules/talent.md#payroll-settings-and-allowances-build-step-23), [Payroll computation](COMPLIANCE.md#payroll-computation-build-step-29), [DATA_MODEL.md](DATA_MODEL.md#collection-ownership) ([Money](DATA_MODEL.md#money), [Versioned configuration](DATA_MODEL.md#versioned-configuration)), [Sensitive data](../SECURITY.md#sensitive-data), [Background jobs](ARCHITECTURE.md#background-jobs), [Payroll runs on a phone](DESIGN_SYSTEM.md#layout--components), [Payroll run tests](TESTING.md#payroll-run-tests).

### 2.9 build (2026-10-19)

Built in the planned chunks (A and B in parallel, then C and F, then D and E), by separate agents, then verified by a separate agent on 2026-10-19 (also in [TESTING › Payroll run tests](TESTING.md#payroll-run-tests)):

- **Verification results (2026-10-19).** Typecheck and lint pass; Prettier passes on the 50 changed and new non-Markdown files. The full `pnpm test` passes, 95 files and 1,685 tests (it started before the last fixes; afterwards the payroll suites, 3 files and 59 tests, and the reveal and worker suites, 4 files and 50 tests, pass again). `pnpm --filter @pulse/web build` passes. `pnpm seed:admin` added `talent.payrollRunSettings` (1) on its first run and nothing on its second. `pnpm dev:payroll-drafts`, with and without `--date`, printed "Go-live date: not set, so nothing was done" (the development database has no go-live date), so draft creation is covered by the tests only; a bad `--date` is refused; `pnpm dev:leave-credits` still works. Hand arithmetic spot checks of P1, P6 and P12 match. Performance: the recompute tests take under a second, and the queries grow with the number of employees only.
- **Defects found and fixed in verification.**
  - **(a) Security: a draft line could be revealed outside the run page.** The generic sensitive reveal on `talent.payrollRunEmployee` (`netPay`, `gross`) let the employee (the own-record rule) or Accounting reveal a draft line's figures without the run page. `loadValue` in `packages/talent/src/server/sensitive/reveals.ts` now returns nothing unless the line's run is finalized (test added; item 284).
  - **(b) The breakdown refuses a line from another run first.** `getPayrollRunBreakdown` takes an optional `{ runId }` and refuses a line from another run before any `reveal` is written; `getPayrollBreakdownAction` passes it (test added). This closes the gap item 282 recorded.
  - **(c) A hardcoded region.** The `'NCR'` fallback in `server/payroll-runs/compute.ts` is now `DEFAULT_WORK_REGION` (item 285).

Worth knowing:

- **The pure pay engine** is `packages/talent/src/payroll-run/` (kinds and labels, the input and breakdown types, one cut-off's lines in `period.ts` and `line.ts`, the earlier periods in `periods.ts`, exact ratios in `exact.ts`, the breakdown's JSON shape version 1 in `breakdown.ts`), tested from hand-built inputs with P1–P12 and H2 in `payroll-run.test.ts`. It reads `dtr/`, `timesheets/`, `payroll/` and `compensation.ts` without changing them.
- **The run setting** `talent.payrollRunSettings` (`packages/talent/src/payroll/run-settings.ts`) sits beside the payroll settings, not among them, with its own view and "Add version" (`getPayrollRunSettingsView`, `addPayrollRunSettingsVersion`), seeded from 2000-01-01 by `pnpm seed:admin`; the "Payroll run" card is in Payroll settings › Pay schedule (items 258, 266).
- **Write guards** on `payrollRuns` and `payrollRunEmployees` (`server/payroll-runs/model.ts`): a run changes only by a recompute's `$set` and `$inc` filtered on `status: 'draft'` and is never deleted; lines are inserted only for a draft run, through `bulkWrite` `insertOne` or `create()` with the session (`insertMany` is refused, since its middleware can't see the session), and deleted only as a draft run's set. `payrollRuns` also has a unique `number` (items 259, 260; [DATA_MODEL](DATA_MODEL.md#locked-and-append-only-records)).
- **The run service** `server/payroll-runs/`: `compute.ts` loads every source in batches outside the transaction (employees, compensation, allowances, the DTR with approved time off, reimbursements, holidays, setting versions, finalized lines for the reconciliation and the mid-month pairing); `service.ts` creates and recomputes (one transaction, checked against the run's `version`, three attempts; item 271), lists, shows a run, "Show amounts" and the breakdown; `getPayrollRunLine` is a server-only read for steps 2.10–2.14 with no access check (item 275).
- **Reveals.** Each "Show amounts" writes and commits a `reveal` on `talent.payrollRun` (field `amounts`) before any amount is decrypted; the amounts last one page view (a reload or recompute hides them, and showing them again writes another entry). Each breakdown opened writes a `reveal` on `talent.payrollRunEmployee` (field `breakdown`). `talent.payrollRunEmployee` is registered with category `payslip`; until step 2.10 its generic reveal answers only for a finalized run's line, and the breakdown refuses a line from another run before writing its reveal (items 274, 278, 282, 284).
- **The draft job** `talent.payrollDraftRuns` (`server/payroll-runs/job.ts`, cron `0 5 * * *` Asia/Manila) is registered by the worker beside the other Talent jobs; only the draft it creates sends `talent.payrollDraftReady` to HR (item 268). The holidays-changed listener `talent.recomputeDtr` now also calls `recomputeDraftsForDates`. `pnpm dev:payroll-drafts [--date YYYY-MM-DD]` runs the job once in development, as of now or 5:00 AM Manila on the given date, and refuses in production (item 277).
- **Screens.** `/talent/payroll` (the runs list, "Compute draft run"), `/talent/payroll/<runId>` (tiles, people list, "Show amounts", totals, "Recompute") and its breakdown sheet, the "Payroll" button on `/talent` (Talent Read and HR, Accounting or the System Administrator), the navigation and breadcrumbs ("Payroll", "Payroll run"), and the Payroll run card with "Add version".
- **No Core change.** The build's own choices are assumptions 258–285 in [Assumptions to check](#assumptions-to-check); none is marked Legal, and 261 (holiday rules) names counsel beside the accountant.

Not checked: the screens in a browser (no browser automation here); they are in the [hands-on checks](#29-hands-on-checks-by-the-user-signed-in).

### 2.9 hands-on checks (by the user, signed in)

Before these, rerun `pnpm seed:admin` on existing development databases (it adds `talent.payrollRunSettings`; a second run adds nothing). Use a throwaway database, and set the go-live date in Payroll settings › Leave on a cut-off start (2026-09-26).

1. **The "Done when"**: with one Overtime and one Standard employee set up as in cases P1 and P2 (salaries, timesheets and approvals), choose "Compute draft run" for Sep 26 – Oct 10, 2026 on `/talent/payroll`, then "Show amounts" and open each breakdown. Their lines and net pay match P1 and P2 (₱11,595.20 and ₱11,092.74) in [the spec's table](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29).
2. **"Show amounts" is audited.** Each press writes a `reveal` on the run (field `amounts`) visible in `/admin/audit`; reloading hides the amounts again, and showing them again writes another entry.
3. **Opening a breakdown is audited**: a `reveal` on the line (field `breakdown`) for each opening, with no amount in the entry.
4. **Access.** A Talent Read user who isn't HR, Accounting or the System Administrator sees no "Payroll" button on `/talent` and gets no access on `/talent/payroll` and `/talent/payroll/<runId>`; an Accounting user sees runs but no "Compute draft run" or "Recompute".
5. **Recompute.** Change an input (approve a timesheet, add a reimbursement), press "Recompute": the lines change, "Computed <when>" updates, and the audit log has an `update` ("Recomputed") with no amount.
6. **The nightly job**: `pnpm dev:payroll-drafts`, with and without `--date YYYY-MM-DD` (try a day after a cut-off's deadline): it prints the date, the go-live date, the latest ended cut-off and the draft created or why not, then the drafts recomputed; a second run creates nothing; HR gets "Draft payroll for <period> is ready to review" only for the job's draft.
7. **Payroll settings › Pay schedule › Payroll run**: the card shows the seeded choices, and "Add version" saves a new version for Talent Owner and HR or the System Administrator.
8. **Light, dark and phone width** (about 390 px, no sideways scroll): `/talent/payroll` and its "Compute draft run" sheet, the run page with its tiles, people list and totals, the breakdown sheet, and the Payroll run settings card with its "Add version" sheet.
9. **Accounting reads only.** An Accounting user with Talent Read sees the runs, "Show amounts" and the breakdowns (each writing its `reveal`), but no "Compute draft run" or "Recompute", and a direct call of either action is refused.
10. **Employees and the Board are kept out.** An employee and a Board member (Talent Read, but not HR, Accounting or the System Administrator) get no access on `/talent/payroll` and `/talent/payroll/<runId>`, and the generic sensitive reveal of a draft line, their own included, is refused (item 284).
11. **A holiday change recomputes drafts.** Confirm or add a holiday on a date inside a draft run's window: after the holidays-changed job, the draft is recomputed ("Computed <when>" updates and its lines reflect the holiday).
12. **The nightly job with a go-live date.** With the go-live date set and an ended cut-off without a run, `pnpm dev:payroll-drafts` creates one draft and sends HR one "Draft payroll for <period> is ready to review"; a rerun creates no draft and sends nothing, and only recomputes the drafts.

### 2.10 notes

- **Spec.** Written first, on 2026-10-19, with every open question settled by its recommended option (the user wants Phase 2 built autonomously, rates and law included): [Payroll review, approval and payslips (build step 2.10)](modules/talent.md#payroll-review-approval-and-payslips-build-step-210), BUILD_PLAN decisions 350–376, and [Payroll approval and payslips](COMPLIANCE.md#payroll-approval-and-payslips-build-step-210). The unconfirmed choices are assumptions 286–318 below; items 286–294 are marked **Legal** for counsel and the accountant. No rate is added. The build added 319–372 (325, a minimum wage earner's manual entries, marked **Legal**). Built on 2026-10-20 (see [2.10 build](#210-build-2026-10-20)), committed as `4475146`, not pushed.
- **Done when:** a Board member approves a run on a phone, and the employee opens their payslip PDF from self-service (case A1). Check it on a throwaway database with step 2.9's P1 and P2 run for Sep 26 – Oct 10, 2026: HR submits it; a Board member with an e-signature opens `/approvals` on a phone (about 390 px), opens the run, shows amounts and approves; P1's employee opens `/talent/me#payslips` on a phone and opens the PDF (net ₱11,595.20, year-to-date gross ₱13,637.50, tax ₱57.92, contributions ₱921.88).
- **Settled while writing the spec** (worth the user's eye):
  - **Item 284 closed without a Core change** (decision 365). The Board's view while approving is the run page's own audited "Show amounts", checked by Talent against the approval request (`listApprovalsForRecord`'s `allowedDecisions`); the generic `talent.payrollRunEmployee` reveal stays finalized-only, since widening it would need a Board `payslip` case in Core's `canRevealSensitive`.
  - **Finalize in cut-off order, recompute before submit** (decision 361), as step 2.9 asked: submit is refused while any earlier cut-off on or after the first payroll cut-off has no finalized run, and recomputes the run first; if anything changed, the recompute is kept and the submit refused so HR reviews what the Board will see.
  - **Overrides never touch attendance items** (decision 356): a later run settles a finalized period's attendance items against what was paid, so an override there would be paid back. Back pay and negative net pay are HR's manual entries (decision 357).
  - **Manual entries' "old value, new value" audit** (decision 359): the amount is encrypted, so the audit log marks it changed and the values live in the insert-only entry chain, shown to whoever may see amounts.
  - **A returned run is edited like a draft** (decision 351), and a pending run is frozen (the engine has no withdraw).
  - **Payslips are frozen at finalizing** (decision 363): identity, bank account and year-to-date figures are stored on the line in the decision's transaction, and the PDF is rendered from them afterwards by a job (or on first open).
- **What it builds on.**
  - Step 2.9: `packages/talent/src/payroll-run/` (the engine, `breakdown.ts` version 1, `kinds.ts`), `server/payroll-runs/` (`compute.ts`, `service.ts`, `job.ts`, `model.ts` and its guards, `getPayrollRunLine`), the run screens under `apps/web/app/(pulse)/talent/payroll/` and `apps/web/lib/actions/talent-payroll-runs.ts`.
  - Step 2.6: `onboarding/gates.ts` (`includeInPayroll`, `checkOnboardingGate`) and the onboarding status service; the e-signature signer gate. Step 2.3: the minimum wage check. Step 2.1: the employee record's bank account and `sensitiveField()`. Step 2.8: `payrollOpeningEntries`. Steps 2.7 and 2.8: the approval type pattern in `server/timesheets/approval.ts` and `server/time-off/approval.ts`, registered through `registerTalentApprovalTypes()` in `server/change-requests/service.ts`.
  - Core: the approvals engine (`registerApprovalType`, `submitApproval`, the `{ set: 'board' }` approvers, `onSettled`, `outcomeRecipients`, `listApprovalsForRecord`, `isApproverFor`), `registerFileAccess` with `auditView`, `renderPdf` and `saveGeneratedFile` (`@pulse/core/server/exports`, `@pulse/core/server`), `getCompanySettings` and `getCompanyLogoFile`, `notify`, `activeHrUserIds`, `recordAudit`. **No Core change is needed** (decision 375).
- **Build order** (followed as planned; A added `payroll-run/manual.ts`, `flags.ts` and `payslip.ts`; C2 split its service into `server/payroll-review/order.ts`, `submit.ts`, `approval.ts`, `finalize.ts`, `board-view.ts`, `register.ts` and `payslip.ts`, with its tests in `approval.test.ts` and `approval-load.test.ts` beside C1's `review.test.ts`; no `pnpm dev:payslip-pdfs` script, item 359). Each chunk is sized for one sub-agent and owns the files listed; nothing outside its list. Chunks on the same line run in parallel and touch no common file. Pure rules, models, services, worker and UI stay apart. Every agent reads the step's Spec line, [Payroll review, approval and payslips](modules/talent.md#payroll-review-approval-and-payslips-build-step-210) and the [2.9 build](#29-build-2026-10-19) notes.
  1. **In parallel:**
