The pure engine.** Owns `packages/talent/src/payroll-run/` only: the manual entry input type (earning, deduction, override with its switches) and the overridable line keys; applying manual entries and overrides before contributions and tax (taxable earnings and deductions that reduce taxable pay in the compensation for contributions and taxable compensation; after-tax deductions in net pay only; counts as basic salary in basic salary earned); the flags it can derive from its inputs (`netPayBelowZero`, `manualEntryStale`, `belowMinimumWage` from the per-day minimum wage it is given, `onboardingIncomplete` and `timesheetNotApproved` from facts it is given); breakdown version 2 (manual and overridden lines) with version 1 still parsed; the payslip view model (the grouped lines with "Adjustment: <label>" and "Adjusted", gross, deductions, net) and the year-to-date sum (opening entry plus lines); tests in `payroll-run.test.ts` (or a new `manual.test.ts`) for A2, A3, A4, A5's arithmetic and A7. It reads `payroll/`, `onboarding/` and `dtr/` without changing them.
     - **B. The models.** Owns `server/payroll-runs/model.ts` and `model.test.ts` (the new run and line fields, `flagCounts`, `payslip` with its `sensitiveField()`s and validator, the widened guards of decision 371), and a new `server/payroll-review/model.ts` with `model.test.ts` (`payrollManualEntries`, `payrollFlagAcknowledgements`, their indexes, insert-only guards, the run-status check in the write's session, the validator).
  2. **After A and B, in parallel:**
     - **C1. The review service** (one agent, since it shares `server/policy.ts`, `server/index.ts` and `server/testing/fixtures.ts` at this stage). Owns `server/payroll-runs/compute.ts` and `service.ts` (load the live manual entries, the onboarding status and the minimum wage facts; store flags, `flagCounts` and `manualEntryCount`; recompute `draft` and `returned`), `server/payroll-runs/job.ts` and `server/dtr/listener.ts` (the editable statuses), a new `server/payroll-review/manual.ts` (add, edit, remove, the HR staff rule, the audit, the recompute in the same transaction) and `acknowledgements.ts`, `server/policy.ts`, `server/index.ts`, `server/testing/fixtures.ts`, and `server/payroll-review/review.test.ts` (flags, acknowledgements, manual entries, A2–A4, A6, A7, A17, and step 2.9's suite still green).
     - **P. The payslip document.** Owns a new `server/payslips/document.tsx` only (the react-pdf payslip from A's view model and the company details, with Core's `PDF_FONT_FAMILY` and `pdfColors`) and `document.test.ts` (rendered text holds the fields and A1's figures). After A only.
  3. **After C1: C2. Submit, approval and finalize** (one agent). Owns new `server/payroll-review/submit.ts`, `approval.ts` (the `talent.payrollRun` type, `onSettled` approve and return, `outcomeRecipients`), `finalize.ts` (the snapshot, year-to-date, `talent.payslipReady`), `board-view.ts` (the Board's view and "Show amounts") and `register.ts`; the registration line in `server/change-requests/service.ts` (`registerTalentApprovalTypes()`); `getPayslip`; `server/policy.ts`, `server/index.ts`, `server/testing/fixtures.ts` (now C2's); and the approval half of `review.test.ts` (A1's approval, A5, A8–A14, A16, A18) through Core's real engine.
  4. **After C2, in parallel:**
     - **C3. Payslips.** Owns new `server/payslips/service.ts` (render with P's document, `saveGeneratedFile`, set the file id once, lazy render on open, the self-service list), `server/payslips/job.ts` (`PAYSLIP_PDFS_JOB`, `runPayslipPdfs(at)`), `server/files/registrations.ts` (`talent.payslip` with `auditView`), `server/payslips/payslips.test.ts` (A1's payslip, A15, the job twice), and only the export lines for these in `server/index.ts` (C2 is done with it).
     - **E1. The run screens.** Owns `apps/web/app/(pulse)/talent/payroll/` (status badges, flag tiles and badges, "Acknowledge" and "Acknowledge all" sheets, the manual entries group, the manual entry sheet and its History, "Submit for approval" and its sheet, the returned callout, the pending and finalized states, the Board's read-only view, the new `[runId]/register/page.tsx`), `apps/web/lib/actions/talent-payroll-runs.ts`, and `apps/web/lib/navigation.ts` with its test ("Payroll register"). It calls C1's and C2's services only.
  5. **After C3, in parallel:**
     - **D. Worker.** Owns `apps/worker/src/talent-jobs.ts` and its test (`talent.payslipPdfs`, cron `*/10 * * * *`).
     - **E2. Self-service payslips.** Owns the Payslips section in `apps/web/app/(pulse)/talent/me/` (a new `own-payslips.tsx` and its wiring in `page.tsx` or `own-record.tsx`) and a new `apps/web/lib/actions/talent-payslips.ts` ("Open payslip" renders if needed and returns the file route URL).
  6. **Verification** (a separate agent): typecheck, lint, Prettier, `pnpm test`, the production build, `pnpm seed:admin` twice (nothing new to seed), `pnpm dev:payroll-drafts`, and a review of: A1–A18 to the centavo, no amount or account number in any log, audit entry, notification or approval title, each `reveal` written before anything is sent, the guards refusing every write outside decision 371, a Board member without an e-signature refused, HR refused on HR staff's manual entries, the PDF opening only for the right people, and every screen at phone width in light and dark.
  7. **Docs:** HANDOFF (2.10 build, hands-on checks, the build's own assumptions from 319), REVIEW_LIST (from R-295), BUILD_SUMMARY, the step's results in TESTING, CHANGELOG, and AGENTS.md (the payroll review and payslip tests in the `pnpm test` list); then commit, not push.
- **Risks.**
  - **The approve hook is heavy.** Finalizing decrypts and re-encrypts the bank account, sums year-to-date over the year's finalized lines and sends one notification per employee, all inside the Board member's decision transaction (MongoDB's default transaction limit is 60 seconds). Load everything in batches before the writes; if it is still slow for the headcount, move the year-to-date sum to the payslip job (the payslip is then frozen when rendered, not at approval) as a spec change.
  - **Each manual entry save recomputes the whole run** (decision 358), slow with many employees and many entries. If it is, recompute only that employee's line and the run totals in the same transaction, as a spec change.
  - **Board members need e-signatures**, or the signer gate refuses their approval; and with no active Board member nobody can approve a payroll (decision 362). Both are business risks before go-live.
  - **No withdraw.** A run sent by mistake waits for a Board member to return it; Core's engine has no withdraw, and step 2.10 adds no Core change.
  - **Item 284** is closed by the run page's own check, not Core's reveal rules (decision 365). If the owner wants the Board to reveal single lines through the generic Reveal, Core's `canRevealSensitive` needs a context case: a Core change.
  - **Payslip PDFs in the worker** need `@react-pdf/renderer` and the Inter fonts at runtime there; Core's `pdf.tsx` already sets its JSX runtime for `tsx`, but the worker hasn't rendered a PDF before.
  - Every pay reading rests on step 2.9's assumptions 225–285 and this step's 286–318; the accountant and counsel check them before the first real payroll (R-237).
- **Spec:** [Payroll review, approval and payslips](modules/talent.md#payroll-review-approval-and-payslips-build-step-210), [Manual inputs & adjustments](modules/talent.md#manual-inputs--adjustments), [Payroll run & payslips](modules/talent.md#payroll-run--payslips), [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29), [Onboarding checklist and change requests](modules/talent.md#onboarding-checklist-and-change-requests-build-step-26), [Approvals](modules/core.md#approvals), [Company details](modules/core.md#company-details-pending-from-the-client), [Payroll approval and payslips](COMPLIANCE.md#payroll-approval-and-payslips-build-step-210), [DATA_MODEL.md](DATA_MODEL.md#collection-ownership) ([Locked and append-only records](DATA_MODEL.md#locked-and-append-only-records)), [Sensitive data](../SECURITY.md#sensitive-data), [Exceptions to module access](../SECURITY.md#exceptions-to-module-access), [File storage](ARCHITECTURE.md#file-storage), [Background jobs](ARCHITECTURE.md#background-jobs), [Payroll review and payslips on a phone](DESIGN_SYSTEM.md#layout--components), [Payroll review and payslip tests](TESTING.md#payroll-review-and-payslip-tests).

### 2.10 build (2026-10-20)

Built in the planned chunks (A and B in parallel, then C1 and P, then C2, then C3 and E1, then D and E2), by separate agents, then verified by a separate agent on 2026-10-20 (also in [TESTING › Payroll review and payslip tests](TESTING.md#payroll-review-and-payslip-tests)):

- **Verification results (2026-10-21).** Typecheck and lint pass; Prettier passes on the 52 changed and new non-Markdown files; the full `pnpm test` passes, 101 files and 1,786 tests, with cases A1–A18 and the Done when (A1); the production build (`next build`) passes; `pnpm seed:admin` ran twice and added nothing (every base record kept, the System Administrator already there); `pnpm dev:payroll-drafts` ran but the development database has no go-live date, so it created nothing (submitting, finalizing and payslips are covered by the tests only). The diff was reviewed against the spec, the Done when and AGENTS.md's "Before finishing a change": every new page and Server Action checks access on the server (the run page's approver exception goes through `getPayrollRunApproverView`, which refuses non-approvers, and refuses "Show amounts" before any `reveal` unless the viewer is a pending approver; the register page and action need Talent Read with HR, Accounting or the System Administrator; "Open payslip" needs only a signed-in user, and `openPayslipHref` answers the same for a missing, unfinalized or refused line); every mutation re-checks the actor in its transaction and writes its audit entry there; amounts are whole centavos through Core's `pesoField`; the run guard allows only the five write shapes, so a pending run is frozen and a finalized run never changes but `payslipsPending`; the payslip line fields are set once; no amount, name or account number is logged (the job's error carries counts only); payslip PDFs are saved with `saveGeneratedFile` and served only through the file route with an audited `reveal`; nothing from steps 2.11+ was found.
- **Defects found and fixed in verification.** None in the code. One doc correction: "No Core change" now names the two `turbopackIgnore` hints in Core's `pdf.tsx` (below and in the Phase 2 status row).

Worth knowing:

- **The pure engine** (`packages/talent/src/payroll-run/`): `manual.ts` applies manual earnings, deductions and overrides before contributions and tax, with the overridable line keys and the form's parse (items 324–327, 332); `flags.ts` derives the five review flags and their counts (item 329); `payslip.ts` builds the payslip's view model and the year-to-date sum (items 330, 331); `breakdown.ts` writes version 2 and still reads version 1 (item 328). Cases A2–A5 and A7's arithmetic are in `payroll-run.test.ts`.
- **Models** (`server/payroll-runs/model.ts`, widened; `server/payroll-review/model.ts`, new): the run's review, submit, return, finalize and payslip fields with the guard's exact shapes (item 319), the line's flags, manual entry count and encrypted payslip snapshot with its one-shot writes (items 320, 323), and the insert-only `payrollManualEntries` and `payrollFlagAcknowledgements` (one acknowledgement per run, employee and flag is a unique index; items 321, 322; [DATA_MODEL](DATA_MODEL.md#collection-ownership)).
- **The review service** (`server/payroll-review/manual.ts`, `acknowledgements.ts`; `server/payroll-runs/compute.ts` and `service.ts`): every compute stores each line's flags, `flagCounts` and `manualEntryCount`; `draft` and `returned` runs are recomputed by HR, the nightly job and the holidays-changed listener; each manual entry save recomputes the whole run in its transaction, retried on a version race (items 338–345).
- **Submit, approval and finalize** (`order.ts`, `submit.ts`, `approval.ts`, `finalize.ts`): the cut-off order checked at submit and again in the approve hook, the recompute at submit, the flag and Board checks (items 346, 347); the approval type `talent.payrollRun`, registered in `registerTalentApprovalTypes()` (item 348); the approve hook freezes every line's payslip snapshot in one `bulkWrite` with year-to-date from the year's finalized lines, about 0.6 s for 50 employees on the test machine (items 349–351).
- **The Board's view and the register** (`board-view.ts`, `register.ts`): the run page picks the payroll view for HR, Accounting and the System Administrator with Talent Read, and the Board's read-only view for an approver of the run; "Show amounts" there is refused before any `reveal` unless the viewer is a pending approver; the register sends only bank group counts until its audited "Show register" (items 352, 353, 364).
- **Payslips** (`server/payroll-review/payslip.ts`, `server/payslips/`): `getPayslip` is server-only with the account's last 4 digits (item 354); `document.tsx` is the react-pdf payslip (one A4 page in Inter, no company TIN, a WebP logo left out; items 334–336, 355); `service.ts` renders on first open or by the job and sets the file id once (items 356, 358); `job.ts` is `talent.payslipPdfs` (`*/10 * * * *`), registered by the worker (items 357, 359). Talent now depends on `@react-pdf/renderer` 4.9.0 and `react` 19.3.0 (item 337). The worker renders PDFs from source with `tsx`; if it is ever bundled, the Inter font files must ship with it (item 360).
- **Screens.** `/talent/payroll/<runId>` with status badges, flag tiles, "Needs review before submitting" with "Acknowledge" and "Acknowledge all", the manual entries group and sheet with History, "Submit for approval" and its sheet, the returned callout, the pending and finalized states, and the Board's read-only view (items 361–368); `/talent/payroll/<runId>/register` (item 369); the Payslips section on `/talent/me#payslips` with "Net pay ••••" and Reveal, and "Open payslip" (items 370–372).
- **"Open payslip" downloads the PDF in the same tab**, not in a new tab as DESIGN_SYSTEM first said: the file route sends every non-image file as an attachment, and a window opened after the server round trip is blocked on phones (item 371).
- **No Core change** to behaviour: the only Core edit is two `/* turbopackIgnore: true */` hints on the `require.resolve` calls in `packages/core/src/server/exports/pdf.tsx` (the Inter font files and `tokens.css`), so the web app's server bundle leaves those run-time lookups to Node when a payslip renders on "Open payslip". The build's own choices are assumptions 319–372 in [Assumptions to check](#assumptions-to-check); 325 is marked **Legal**.

Not checked: the screens in a browser and the PDF on a phone (no browser automation here); they are in the [hands-on checks](#210-hands-on-checks-by-the-user-signed-in).

### 2.10 hands-on checks (by the user, signed in)

Before these, rerun `pnpm seed:admin` on existing development databases (step 2.10 seeds nothing new; it adds whatever earlier steps left missing). Use a throwaway database with the go-live date set in Payroll settings › Leave on a cut-off start (2026-09-26), step 2.9's P1 and P2 run for Sep 26 – Oct 10, 2026, and two Board members. **Every Board member needs an e-signature**, or the approval is refused; the worker must be running for the payslip job.

1. **The "Done when"** (case A1), on a phone (about 390 px) end to end: HR submits the run; a Board member opens `/approvals`, opens the run, chooses "Show amounts" and approves; P1's employee follows "Your payslip for Sep 26 – Oct 10 is ready" to `/talent/me#payslips` on a phone and opens the PDF (net ₱11,595.20, year-to-date gross ₱13,637.50, tax ₱57.92, contributions ₱921.88). `/admin/audit` shows both reveals: the Board member's on the run (field `amounts`) and the employee's on the line (field `file`).
2. **Manual entries.** On an employee's breakdown: add an earning, a deduction and an override; edit one and remove one, each with a reason; the breakdown's History shows the earlier values; the audit log has `create`, `update` and `delete` on `talent.payrollManualEntry` with the reason and no amount. An after-tax deduction is refused until "Authorized in writing by the employee or required by law" is ticked (A3); an override of "Absences" is refused (A4).
3. **HR staff's manual entries.** HR is refused on an HR employee's line and on their own; the System Administrator's entry is saved (A17).
4. **Acknowledgements.** Under "Needs review before submitting", "Acknowledge" with a reason and "Acknowledge all" on a flag's tile; "Net pay below zero" and "Manual entry needs review" offer no "Acknowledge" (A6, A7).
5. **Submit refusals.** An earlier cut-off not finalized (A8: "Finalize … first."); a flag not resolved or acknowledged (A6); no active Board member (A11); a change found by the recompute at submit (A9: "The run changed when it was recomputed just now…"). Each refusal changes nothing else.
6. **Return and resubmit** (A10): a Board member returns with remarks; HR and the other HR users are notified; the run shows the remarks in a callout and can be edited; HR submits again and another Board member approves.
7. **The Board's view on a phone** (A12): read-only, "Manual entries" first, flags and acknowledgements, and after "Show amounts" the totals, each net pay and each manual amount; no breakdown, bank account, register or payslip; a Board member who isn't an approver of the run gets no access; after the decision, no amounts.
8. **The register** (A16): on a finalized run, "Payroll register" shows the bank groups and counts; "Show register" writes a `reveal` (field `register`) and then shows the full account numbers and net pay, grouped by bank, with each bank's total; before finalizing it says it isn't available; a Talent Read user who isn't HR, Accounting or the System Administrator gets "No access".
9. **Who opens a payslip** (A15): the employee, HR, Accounting and the System Administrator open it, each writing a `reveal` (field `file`); another employee, a Board member as such and a supervisor get "File not found.".
10. **The payslip PDF on a phone**: readable at phone width, with the company header, the spec's fields, "Adjustment: <label>" and "Adjusted" lines, the year-to-date block, and "Credited to <bank> ending <last 4>"; never the full account number, an employer share or a rate. "Open payslip" downloads it in the same tab (item 371).
11. **Self-service list**: newest first; "Net pay ••••" with Reveal writes a `reveal` (field `netPay`); the notification's link scrolls to the Payslips section.
12. **The payslip job**: after approval the finalized run reads "Payslips: being prepared" and, within about 10 minutes with the worker running, "Payslips: 2 of 2 ready".
13. **A finalized run is locked** (A13, A18): recompute, manual entries and acknowledgements are refused, and the page shows "Pulse Fiscal posting: waiting for Pulse Fiscal (Phase 5)."
14. **Light, dark and phone width** (about 390 px, no sideways scroll) on every new screen: the run page in each status, the flag and acknowledgement sheets, the manual entry sheet, the submit sheet, the Board's view, the register, and the Payslips section on `/talent/me`.

### 2.11 notes

- **Spec.** Written first, on 2026-10-25, with every open question settled by its recommended option (the user wants Phase 2 built autonomously, rates, law and formats included): [Employer contributions and remittances (build step 2.11)](modules/talent.md#employer-contributions-and-remittances-build-step-211), BUILD_PLAN decisions 377–400, and [Remittances](COMPLIANCE.md#remittances-build-step-211). The unconfirmed choices are assumptions 373–398 below; items 373–376, 381, 382 and 386 are marked **Legal** for counsel and the accountant, and 378–380 are **Format** items to check against each agency's official file specification. No rate is added. The build added 399–414. Built on 2026-10-25 (see [2.11 build](#211-build-2026-10-25)), committed as `1b595b9` and pushed; independent verification pending.
- **Done when:** a month's SSS report matches its finalized runs, and recording the payment marks SSS paid for that month (cases R1 and R2). Check it on a throwaway database with step 2.10's October 2026 setting: PAY-2026-0001 (Sep 26 – Oct 10) and PAY-2026-0002 (Oct 11 – 25) finalized; HR generates SSS for October 2026 and sees employee ₱2,375.00, employer ₱4,750.00, EC ₱60.00, amount due ₱7,185.00, each equal to the two runs' SSS lines; HR records the payment (Nov 27, 2026, a reference, ₱7,185.00) and `/talent/remittances` shows SSS October 2026 "Paid".
- **The agency file specifications weren't obtained.** BUILD_PLAN asks for them before this step. A web search on 2026-10-25 found the processes (SSS's R-3 through the R3 File Generator or the My.SSS e-CL and a PRN; PhilHealth's EPRS with RF-1; Pag-IBIG's eSRS made mandatory by Circular No. 499-2026; BIR 1601-C typed into eFPS or eBIRForms) but no field layout from any agency. So, as recommended: each upload format is one isolated, versioned definition marked **not verified**, built from the public descriptions, with the export warning HR; an official layout replaces it as a new definition (decision 388). BIR's alphalist format for step 2.12 was found in part (the Alphalist Data Entry and Validation Module v7.x; RMC No. 7-2021 Annexes A and B, reiterated by RMC No. 25-2024), recorded in [COMPLIANCE › Inputs to get before building](COMPLIANCE.md#inputs-to-get-before-building).
- **Settled while writing the spec** (worth the user's eye):
  - **A report is what the runs hold** (decision 379): the sums of the finalized lines, overrides included, never recomputed from the tables, so the Done when holds by construction and a wrong deduction shows as "Differs from the schedule" and is corrected in a later run.
  - **The earliest due date is seeded** (decision 384): PhilHealth the 15th (not the 20th), Pag-IBIG the 14th (Circular 275's earliest window; Circular 499-2026's schedule wasn't found), BIR the 10th (not the eFPS group date). HR sets the company's own dates.
  - **A snapshot per agency and month** (decision 385), regenerated freely until paid and locked once paid; a void unlocks it. Exports render from the snapshot and are stored per version.
  - **One Core change** (decision 389): `saveGeneratedFile` accepts `text/plain` and `text/csv`, for the upload files. Everything else Core already has.
  - **Accounting records payments with Talent Write** (decision 394), since recording is a write; in steps 2.9 and 2.10 Accounting only reads.
  - **The go-live month** may hold only the cut-off paid in Pulse (decision 378), with a note; HR completes it from the old system.
- **What it builds on.**
  - Steps 2.9 and 2.10: the finalized runs and lines (`server/payroll-runs/model.ts`, the stored breakdown's `contributions` with `mscCentavos`, `employeeRegularCentavos`, `employeeMpfCentavos` and each agency's `basisCentavos`; the line kinds in `payroll-run/kinds.ts`; `getPayrollRunLine`), `server/payroll-review/order.ts` (the cut-offs in order, for completeness), the run's `settingVersions` (the SSS table version). Step 2.3: `server/payroll-settings/` (`settings.ts`, `seed-data.ts`, the "Add version" pattern and the Pay schedule segment). Steps 2.1 and 2.6: the employee record's government IDs, date of birth and sex, and the verification facts. Step 2.7: the reminder job and dev script pattern (`server/timesheets/` reminders, `scripts/dev-timesheet-reminders.ts`). Step 2.10: the "Show amounts" reveal and the file route registration (`server/files/registrations.ts`, `auditView`).
  - Core: `renderReportPdf`, `buildReportWorkbook` and the `Report` description (`@pulse/core/server/exports`), `saveGeneratedFile` and `GENERATED_TYPES` (`packages/core/src/server/files/file-types.ts`, `stored-files.ts`), `getCompanySettings`, `isPlaceholder` and `COMPANY_DETAIL_FIELDS` (`sssEmployerNumber`, `philhealthEmployerNumber`, `pagibigEmployerId`, `tin`, `rdoCode`), `notify`, `activeHrUserIds`, `activeSystemAdministrators`, `recordAudit` (`reveal`, `export`), `registerFileAccess`.
- **Build order.** Each chunk is sized for one sub-agent and owns the files listed; nothing outside its list. Chunks on the same line run in parallel and touch no common file. Every agent reads the step's Spec line, [Employer contributions and remittances](modules/talent.md#employer-contributions-and-remittances-build-step-211), [Remittances](COMPLIANCE.md#remittances-build-step-211) and the [2.10 build](#210-build-2026-10-20) notes.
  1. **In parallel:**
     - **A. The pure engine.** Owns a new `packages/talent/src/remittance/` only: `REMITTANCE_AGENCIES`; the remittance months and completeness from the cut-offs and the go-live date; due dates from a settings value (a day past the month's end, `monthsAfter`, December); the report builder from hand-built lines and identities (rows, the SS and MPF split, the basis and salary credit, the flags, the totals and amount due, the 1601-C items and the item 22 check); the snapshot types with `rowsVersion` 1; and `formats/` with the three definitions (`sss.r3.assumed-2026-10`, `philhealth.rf1.assumed-2026-10`, `pagibig.mcrf.assumed-2026-10`), each a pure `build(snapshot)` with `verified: false`, and the ASCII and amount formatting. Tests in `remittance.test.ts` for R1, R3, R5, R11–R13 and R15's arithmetic. It reads `payroll-run/` and `payroll/` without changing them.
     - **B. The models and the setting.** Owns a new `server/remittances/model.ts` and `model.test.ts` (`remittanceReports` and `remittancePayments`: fields, indexes, `sensitiveField()`s with the validators, the guards of DATA_MODEL), `talent.remittanceSettings` in `server/payroll-settings/settings.ts` and `seed-data.ts` (shape, validation, seed from 2000-01-01) with its tests in `payroll-settings.test.ts`, and the seed loader line in `server/seed/index.ts`.
     - **K. The Core change.** Owns `packages/core/src/server/files/file-types.ts`, the generated type check in `stored-files.ts` and their tests: `text/plain` (`.txt`) and `text/csv` (`.csv`), accepted only as printable ASCII with CR LF line ends. Nothing else in Core.
  2. **After A, B and K: C. The service** (one agent). Owns a new `server/remittances/` `service.ts` (the months and statuses, `getRemittanceStatus`), `generate.ts` (completeness, reading the source lines and identities, the snapshot, regenerate, the audit), `exports.ts` (the `Report` description, PDF, Excel and the upload file, `saveGeneratedFile`, the `export` entry, the refusals for a missing ID or a placeholder employer number), `payments.ts` (record, the amount warning, correct, void, the audit, the Fiscal placeholder), `view.ts` ("Show amounts" with its `reveal`); `server/files/registrations.ts` (`talent.remittanceReport`, `auditView`); `server/policy.ts`, `server/index.ts`, `server/testing/fixtures.ts`; and `remittances.test.ts` (R1, R2, R4–R9, R12, R14, R15 through step 2.10's real approval).
  3. **After C, in parallel:**
     - **D. The job, the worker and the dev script.** Owns a new `server/remittances/job.ts` (`REMITTANCE_REMINDERS_JOB`, `runRemittanceReminders(at)`) and `job.test.ts` (R10), `apps/worker/src/talent-jobs.ts` and its test (cron `0 8 * * *`), a new `scripts/dev-remittance-reminders.ts` and its `package.json` script (refuses in production, like `scripts/dev-timesheet-reminders.ts`), and only the export line for the job in `server/index.ts` (C is done with it).
     - **E. The screens.** Owns a new `apps/web/app/(pulse)/talent/remittances/` (the list, the report page, the exports, the payment sheets), a new `apps/web/lib/actions/talent-remittances.ts`, the "Remittances" button on `/talent`, the Remittances card in `apps/web/app/(pulse)/talent/payroll-settings/` (Pay schedule segment), and `apps/web/lib/navigation.ts` with its test ("Remittances", "Remittance"). It calls C's services only.
  4. **Verification** (a separate agent): typecheck, lint, Prettier, `pnpm test`, the production build, `pnpm seed:admin` twice (it adds `talent.remittanceSettings` once), `pnpm dev:remittance-reminders` with and without `--date`, and a review of: R1–R15 to the centavo, no amount or ID in any log, audit entry or notification, each `reveal` and `export` written before anything is sent, the guards refusing every other write, the upload refusals, the not-verified warning on every upload export, and every screen at phone width in light and dark.
  5. **Docs:** HANDOFF (2.11 build, hands-on checks, the build's own assumptions from 399), REVIEW_LIST (from R-358), BUILD_SUMMARY, the step's results in TESTING, CHANGELOG, and AGENTS.md (`pnpm dev:remittance-reminders` in Commands, the remittance settings in `pnpm seed:admin`, the remittance tests in the `pnpm test` list); then commit, not push.
- **Risks.**
  - **The upload files will probably be rejected** by the agencies' portals until their real layouts are obtained (items 378–380). PDF and Excel are dependable; the upload file is a convenience until verified. Get the specifications (R-337 to R-339) before the first real remittance.
  - **The due dates may be wrong**, especially Pag-IBIG's under Circular No. 499-2026 (item 375). They are seeded early on purpose, but HR must set the company's own before relying on the reminders.
  - **Go-live month.** If go-live lands on an end-of-month cut-off, that month's remittance in Pulse is only half; HR must add the old system's part (item 384).
  - **Generating decrypts every line of the month** (two runs × the headcount) and every employee's IDs in one request; fine for the company's size, but keep it out of a transaction and write the snapshot in one.
  - **Exported files are kept forever** with full IDs (item 386), as payslips are; retention is R-036.
  - Every figure rests on steps 2.9 and 2.10's pay assumptions (225–372); the accountant checks them before the first real payroll (R-237).
- **Spec:** [Employer contributions and remittances](modules/talent.md#employer-contributions-and-remittances-build-step-211), [Employer contributions & monthly remittances](modules/talent.md#employer-contributions--monthly-remittances), [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29), [Payroll review, approval and payslips](modules/talent.md#payroll-review-approval-and-payslips-build-step-210), [Company details](modules/core.md#company-details-pending-from-the-client), [Remittances](COMPLIANCE.md#remittances-build-step-211), [DATA_MODEL.md](DATA_MODEL.md#collection-ownership) ([Versioned configuration](DATA_MODEL.md#versioned-configuration), [Locked and append-only records](DATA_MODEL.md#locked-and-append-only-records)), [Sensitive data](../SECURITY.md#sensitive-data), [Audit logging](../SECURITY.md#audit-logging), [File storage](ARCHITECTURE.md#file-storage), [Background jobs](ARCHITECTURE.md#background-jobs), [Placeholders during the staged build](ARCHITECTURE.md#placeholders-during-the-staged-build), [Remittances on a phone](DESIGN_SYSTEM.md#layout--components), [Remittance tests](TESTING.md#remittance-tests).

### 2.11 build (2026-10-25)

Built in the planned order (A, B and K, then C, then D and E) by one agent on 2026-10-25; verification is left to a separate agent.

- **Verification results.** Independent verification was interrupted and is still pending: the production build, `pnpm seed:admin` twice, `pnpm dev:remittance-reminders` and a code review weren't done. The build agent's own results: typecheck, lint, Prettier and `pnpm test` (107 files, 1,869 tests) passed. Committed at the user's request before verification.

Worth knowing:

- **The pure engine** (`packages/talent/src/remittance/`): `agencies.ts` (the four agencies, statuses, months and every word the screens, exports and job use), `settings.ts` (`talent.remittanceSettings`'s schema, seed and `remittanceDueDate`), `months.ts` (each month's cut-offs by pay date, the go-live month's cut-offs paid before go-live, completeness), `report.ts` (the rows from the lines' kinds, the SS and MPF split, the flags, the totals and the 1601-C items 14–25 with the item 22 check), `types.ts` (the snapshot, `rowsVersion` 1) and `formats/` (the three isolated definitions, each `verified: false`, and the ASCII, CR LF and amount rules). Cases R1, R3, R5, R11–R13 and R15's arithmetic are in `remittance.test.ts`, from hand-built lines with steps 2.9 and 2.10's figures.
- **Models** (`server/remittances/model.ts`): `remittanceReports` (unique per agency and month; the guard allows exactly the five update shapes of DATA_MODEL) and the insert-only `remittancePayments` (the partial unique indexes keep one chain per report). The rows, totals and payment amounts are `sensitiveField()`s with the `$jsonSchema` backstop.
- **The setting** (`talent.remittanceSettings`): declared beside the payroll run settings in `server/payroll-settings/settings.ts`, seeded from 2000-01-01 by a new loader in `pnpm seed:admin`, with its view and "Add version" in `server/payroll-settings/service.ts` (the step 2.3 rules).
- **The one Core change** (`packages/core/src/server/files/file-types.ts`, `stored-files.ts`): `GENERATED_TYPES` gains `text/plain` (`.txt`) and `text/csv` (`.csv`), and `saveGeneratedFile` accepts them only as printable ASCII lines each ended with CR LF (`isPrintableAsciiCrLf`). Tested in `generated-files.test.ts`.
- **The service** (`server/remittances/`): `sources.ts` (the context, each month's cut-offs and runs), `service.ts` (statuses, `/talent/remittances`, the report page, `getRemittanceStatus` for steps 2.16 and 5.8), `generate.ts` (generate and regenerate: the lines, identities and IDs read outside the transaction, the snapshot written in one), `exports.ts` and `document.tsx` (the `Report` description, the A4 landscape PDF, the Excel file and the upload file; the `export` entry first, one file per version and format), `payments.ts` (record with the amount warning, correct, void), `view.ts` ("Show amounts", the `reveal` first) and `snapshot.ts`. The file route's `talent.remittanceReport` check (only a file the report's current `files` points at, with `auditView`) is in `server/files/registrations.ts`; the policy functions in `server/policy.ts`.
- **The PDF isn't Core's `renderReportPdf`.** That renders portrait with nothing after the table, and the spec asks for A4 landscape with the due date, payment status and notes after it; changing it would be a second Core change. So `server/remittances/document.tsx` lays out the same `Report` description with Core's `renderPdf`, fonts, colors and `formatCell` (item 399). The Excel file is Core's `buildReportWorkbook`, with the employer's details, due date and warnings in its subtitle.
- **The job** (`server/remittances/job.ts`): `talent.remittanceReminders` (`0 8 * * *`), registered by the worker; `pnpm dev:remittance-reminders [--date YYYY-MM-DD]` (`scripts/dev-remittance-reminders.ts`) runs it once in development and refuses in production. Tested in `job.test.ts` (R10).
- **Screens.** `/talent/remittances` (months newest first, four agencies each), `/talent/remittances/<YYYY-MM>/<agency>` (status, due date, source runs, callouts, tiles, "Show amounts", exports with the upload sheet, the payment section and its sheets), the "Remittances" button on `/talent`, and the Remittances card with "Add version" in Payroll settings › Pay schedule. `apps/web/lib/actions/talent-remittances.ts` holds the Server Actions; the settings action is in `talent-payroll.ts`. Navigation: "Pulse Talent › Remittances" and "Pulse Talent › Remittance".
- **Tests added:** `remittance/remittance.test.ts` (the pure engine and formats), `server/remittances/model.test.ts`, `remittances.test.ts` (R1–R4, R6–R9, R12–R14 end to end, October 2026's two runs finalized through step 2.10's real approval), `go-live.test.ts` (R15, and R5 end to end with a real override), `job.test.ts` (R10), Core's `generated-files.test.ts`, and additions to `payroll-settings.test.ts`, `apps/worker/src/talent-jobs.test.ts` and `apps/web/lib/navigation.test.ts`.
- **The build's own choices** are assumptions 399–414 in [Assumptions to check](#assumptions-to-check).

Not checked: the screens in a browser, the PDF and Excel files by eye, and the upload files against any agency portal (no browser automation here, and no agency specification); they are in the [hands-on checks](#211-hands-on-checks-by-the-user-signed-in).

### 2.11 hands-on checks (by the user, signed in)

Before these, rerun `pnpm seed:admin` (it adds `talent.remittanceSettings` once; a second run adds nothing). Use a throwaway database with step 2.10's setting: the go-live date 2026-09-26, PAY-2026-0001 (Sep 26 – Oct 10) and PAY-2026-0002 (Oct 11 – 25) both finalized through a Board member's approval, each employee's government IDs entered and verified. Sign in as HR (Talent Write) unless a check says otherwise.

1. **The "Done when"** (cases R1 and R2), on a phone (about 390 px): `/talent` › "Remittances" › SSS · October 2026 › "Generate report"; "Show amounts" shows employee ₱2,375.00, employer ₱4,750.00, EC ₱60.00 and amount due ₱7,185.00, and each person's row matches their two payslips' SSS lines; "Record payment" (Nov 27, 2026, "PRN 1234567890123", 7,185.00) then shows "Paid" and "Paid Nov 27, 2026 · PRN 1234567890123" on `/talent/remittances`. `/admin/audit` has the report's `create`, the `reveal` (field `amounts`) and the payment's `create`, none with an amount.
2. **Not ready** (R4): before PAY-2026-0002 is finalized, October's four agencies read "Not ready" and the report page says "Finalize Oct 11 – 25, 2026 first: its pay date is in October 2026."
3. **PhilHealth, Pag-IBIG and BIR 1601-C** (R3, R12): PhilHealth ₱2,392.50 due, Pag-IBIG ₱800.00; BIR 1601-C shows items 14–25 in a list (item 25 the two runs' withholding), "BIR Form 1601-C has no upload file: …" and no upload button.
4. **Exports** (R14): "PDF" downloads an A4 landscape PDF with the employer's details, the table, the totals, then "Due Nov 30, 2026" and the status; "Excel" a one-sheet workbook with the same columns and peso text; each press writes an `export` entry and each open a `reveal` (field `file`) in `/admin/audit`.
5. **The upload file** (R6, R13): the button reads "Upload file (layout not verified)" and its sheet shows the not-verified warning in `warning-text`. With the SSS employer number still a placeholder the report shows the placeholder warning and the upload is refused; fill it in in Company settings, "Regenerate", and the `.txt` file downloads (a header, one line per employee, a trailer). Open it in a text editor: upper-case, fixed columns, CR LF line ends.
6. **A missing ID** (R6): clear one employee's SS number (Talent › the employee › Government IDs; or on the database), "Regenerate": the "Missing or unverified ID" tile reads 1, the row says "No SS number on file", PDF and Excel still export, and the upload sheet says "Enter the SS numbers of 1 employee first."
7. **The amount warning** (R7): record ₱7,200.00: the sheet shows "₱7,200.00 isn't the report's amount due of ₱7,185.00. Save anyway if penalties or another month are included." and saves only on "Save anyway".
8. **Correct and void** (R8, R9): "Correct" with a new reference and a reason saves a new entry (History shows both); "Regenerate" is gone while paid; "Void" with a reason returns it to "Generated", and "Regenerate" and "Record payment" come back. Accounting (Dan) with Talent Read only sees the page and exports but has no payment buttons; with Talent Write he can correct.
9. **Access** (R14): a Talent Read user outside HR and Accounting, a Board member and an employee get "No access" on `/talent/remittances` and a report page, and no "Remittances" button on `/talent`.
10. **Reminders** (R10): with the worker stopped, run `pnpm dev:remittance-reminders --date 2026-11-25` (SSS "soon" to each active HR user), again the same day (nothing new), `--date 2026-11-30` ("due today") and `--date 2026-12-01` ("overdue"); the bell shows each once with no amount, and an agency already paid gets none. Without `--date` it runs as of today; with `NODE_ENV=production` it refuses.
11. **The Remittances card** in Payroll settings › Pay schedule: the seeded version (SSS the last day of the next month, PhilHealth the 15th, Pag-IBIG the 14th, BIR 1601-C the 10th with December's on the 15th, the first reminder 5 days before) with the seeded note; "Add version" (Talent Owner in HR) with a later date saves and shows as scheduled; a Talent Write user doesn't see "Add version".
12. **Light, dark and phone width** (about 390 px, no sideways scroll) on every new screen: the remittances list, the report page before and after "Show amounts" (people as a list on a phone, a table on a desktop), the generate, upload, payment, correction and void sheets, and the Remittances card and its sheet.

## Assumptions to check

Things the build assumed that a person still has to confirm or do. Later Phase 2 steps add to this list. Items 27–34 note how far each seeded rate was verified online on 2026-10-12. Nothing here blocks the build. Check each item before HR enters real employee data, or before go-live where it says so. The owner signs off from [REVIEW_LIST.md](REVIEW_LIST.md), which groups these rows (for counsel, the accountant, business decisions, hands-on checks and go-live) with stable R- IDs.

| 1 | **Date of birth and civil status** (RA 10173 Section 3(l): age and marital status are sensitive personal information). They are limited to HR, Accounting, the System Administrator and the employee, kept out of the list, and not encrypted. They appear in audit snapshots, which only the System Administrator can browse. Confirm this is enough, or whether they must be encrypted too. *Assumption, to be confirmed by HR or counsel* | 2.1 | HR, counsel | [Sensitive data](../SECURITY.md#sensitive-data), [COMPLIANCE.md](COMPLIANCE.md#data-privacy-act-of-2012-ra-10173) |

| 40 | **Not seeded:** double holidays (300%), special working days, and the lower minimum wage for agriculture and small retail, service and manufacturing employers (₱718 in NCR), since the spec doesn't list them. *Assumption, to be confirmed by HR or the accountant* | 2.3 | HR, the accountant | [Payroll settings and allowances](modules/talent.md#payroll-settings-and-allowances-build-step-23) |
| 41 | **Versions can't be withdrawn.** A wrong payroll settings version is replaced from a later date ([RUNBOOK](RUNBOOK.md#a-wrong-payroll-settings-version-was-added)). Company policy and the premium rates are seeded from 2000-01-01 so they cover every date hired | 2.3 | The user | [Payroll settings and allowances](modules/talent.md#payroll-settings-and-allowances-build-step-23) |
| 42 | **Allowances and the date hired.** No allowance applies before the date hired, automatic or assigned. An automatic type applies only while it is active in that day's allowance types version. Nothing stops the automatic internet allowance at a separation date yet; the payroll run and final pay steps decide that. *Assumption, to be confirmed by HR* | 2.3 | HR | [Payroll settings and allowances](modules/talent.md#payroll-settings-and-allowances-build-step-23) |
| 43 | **Contribution rounding.** PhilHealth: the premium rounded half-up once, the employee's half rounded half-up, the employer the remainder. Pag-IBIG: each share rounded half-up on its own. The agencies' own rounding wasn't checked. *Assumption, to be confirmed by the accountant* | 2.3 | The accountant | [Payroll settings and allowances](modules/talent.md#payroll-settings-and-allowances-build-step-23) |
| 44 | **The minimum wage warning shows the daily rate rounded down** to the centavo (₱16,421.24 a month shows ₱754.99 against ₱755.00), so it never reads as the minimum itself. The comparison stays exact. Changed in the review | 2.3 | HR | [Payroll settings and allowances](modules/talent.md#payroll-settings-and-allowances-build-step-23) |
| 45 | **Edge cases left as built:** a monthly salary is refused, not warned, when no pay schedule is on file (only before `pnpm seed:admin` has run). The seed adds a setting's versions only when it has none, so if a run stopped between NCR-26 and NCR-28, a later run wouldn't add NCR-28; HR would add it on the page. A version counts as "Seeded" when its date and source match the seed's exactly | 2.3 | The user | [Payroll settings and allowances](modules/talent.md#payroll-settings-and-allowances-build-step-23) |
| 46 | **The seeded break is 12:00 to 13:00.** The spec says only "a 1-hour break". The break window is deducted wherever entries overlap it, on any day, even when the employee works through it (unpaid break) | 2.4 | HR | [Work schedules and daily time entries](modules/talent.md#work-schedules-and-daily-time-entries-build-step-24) |
| 47 | **How "beyond the schedule" is measured.** Extra hours on an ordinary day are the worked minutes beyond the scheduled minutes, taken as the day's last minutes. Arriving early counts only through the day's total, and a late start made up at the end of the day earns none (Labor Code Arts. 87–88) | 2.4 | HR, counsel | [Timesheet hours](COMPLIANCE.md#timesheet-hours-build-step-24) |
| 48 | **Rest-day and holiday work on the Overtime timesheet** counts only when the employee marks it with a reason, as the spec says for the Standard timesheet | 2.4 | HR, counsel | [Timesheet hours](COMPLIANCE.md#timesheet-hours-build-step-24) |
| 49 | **Local holidays aren't applied** to anyone yet, since no employee has a work location; they show on the day as information. Only confirmed nationwide holidays count | 2.4 | HR | [Holiday calendar](modules/core.md#holiday-calendar) |
| 50 | **An entry past midnight belongs to the day it started** and takes that day's kind. DOLE practice splits holiday hours by calendar day, so check this before night work on a holiday eve is paid (step 2.9) | 2.4 | HR, the accountant | [Timesheet hours](COMPLIANCE.md#timesheet-hours-build-step-24) |
| 51 | **No minimum overtime block** and no rounding to 15 or 30 minutes. Hours come from exact minutes and show to 0.1 h | 2.4 | HR | [Work schedules and daily time entries](modules/talent.md#work-schedules-and-daily-time-entries-build-step-24) |
| 52 | **Night differential** uses the `talent.premiumRates` night window and `payrollSettings.hoursPerDay`. It counts paid minutes only and shows on the Overtime timesheet only; the DTR (step 2.7) also counts it for Standard employees' paid night hours | 2.4 | HR, the accountant | [Timesheet hours](COMPLIANCE.md#timesheet-hours-build-step-24) |
| 53 | **The offset ratio is 1:1** (seeded `talent.timesheetSettings.offsetRatio` `"1"`), and offset minutes are rounded half-up to the minute | 2.4 | HR | [DATA_MODEL.md](DATA_MODEL.md#versioned-configuration) |
| 54 | **HR can't correct an employee's time entries** in step 2.4. Corrections come through "return with remarks" in step 2.7 | 2.4 | HR | [Exceptions to module access](../SECURITY.md#exceptions-to-module-access) |
| 55 | **A timesheet created after its cut-off started** (a new hire, or a missed job run) takes the position's type on the day it is created, recorded in `typeReadOn` | 2.4 | HR | [Work schedules and daily time entries](modules/talent.md#work-schedules-and-daily-time-entries-build-step-24) |
| 56 | **The offset balance shows 0.0 h** until the credit and usage entries (step 2.7) and the opening balances (step 2.8) exist | 2.4 | HR | [Offset balance & offset time off](modules/talent.md#offset-balance--offset-time-off) |
| 57 | **Clock times come from the server's clock.** Manual times have no limit beyond "not in the future", "no overlap", "at most 24 hours" and the employment dates; approvers see them flagged | 2.4 | HR | [Work schedules and daily time entries](modules/talent.md#work-schedules-and-daily-time-entries-build-step-24) |
| 58 | **Supervisors see their direct reports' timesheets read-only from step 2.4**, outside module access, before they approve them in step 2.7. They get only directory fields about the employee | 2.4 | HR, counsel | [Exceptions to module access](../SECURITY.md#exceptions-to-module-access) |
| 59 | **A supervisor can work out a team member's date hired** when it falls in a cut-off they open: the day list starts at the date hired, the previous cut-off button stops there, and an earlier `?cutOff=` is not found. Accepted, as the supervisor works with the employee | 2.4 | HR, counsel | [Work schedules and daily time entries](modules/talent.md#work-schedules-and-daily-time-entries-build-step-24) |
| 60 | **One concurrency case is left open:** two saves at once (two devices) of overlapping entries on either side of a cut-off boundary, when neither cut-off has a timesheet yet, could both save. | 61 | **Opening one's own past cut-off creates its timesheet** if none exists, with the position's type that day (as in item 55), so browsing back writes timesheets. | 62 | **A merchant TIN with no branch code is the head office** (`00000`), and the branch code is part of the duplicate key, because invoice series are registered per branch | 2.5 | Accountant | [Reimbursement receipts](COMPLIANCE.md#reimbursement-receipts-build-step-25) |
| 63 | **Receipt numbers match ignoring case, separators and leading zeros** (`or-000123` and `OR 123` are the same receipt). The separators go first, then the leading zeros of each run of digits at the start or after a letter, keeping one digit: `SI-0042` is `SI42` and `0000` is `0`, but `2026-0001` is `20260001` (a zero after a digit is kept, even across a dash) | 2.5 | Accountant | [Reimbursement receipts](COMPLIANCE.md#reimbursement-receipts-build-step-25) |
| 64 | **Invoices count as receipts** under RA 11976 (EOPT Act) and RR 7-2024; the field reads "Receipt or invoice number" | 2.5 | Accountant | [Reimbursement receipts](COMPLIANCE.md#reimbursement-receipts-build-step-25) |
| 65 | **The app doesn't check that a receipt is made out to the company**, though that may be needed for the reimbursement to stay non-taxable and deductible | 2.5 | Accountant | [Reimbursement receipts](COMPLIANCE.md#reimbursement-receipts-build-step-25) |
| 66 | **No limits are seeded** for meal, transportation or other; HR adds them as a new `talent.reimbursementSettings` version | 2.5 | HR | [Reimbursements](modules/talent.md#reimbursements-build-step-25) |

| 225 | **Legal (RA 11199, RA 11223, RA 9679): contribution bases.** PhilHealth on the monthly basic salary in effect on the first day of the contribution month (the pay date's month; on the date hired if later; a daily rate counted as daily × the factor ÷ 12); SSS and Pag-IBIG on the month's compensation for contributions (taxable pay less attendance deductions, from both cut-offs paid that month; non-taxable allowances, de minimis within its limit and reimbursements left out). **Rates:** one setting per agency (`talent.payrollRunSettings.contributionBases`), seeded as step 2.3 recommended | 2.9 | The accountant | [Payroll computation](COMPLIANCE.md#payroll-computation-build-step-29) |
| 226 | **Contributions across the two cut-offs.** The mid-month cut-off deducts its split share (half) of the month's contribution on the monthly basic salary, as an estimate for every agency; the end-of-month cut-off deducts the real month less the mid-month deduction, reading the mid-month run whether finalized or draft; a negative remainder is refunded in that cut-off. Employer shares and EC follow the same split | 2.9 | The accountant | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 227 | **No mid-month run in a contribution month** (the first payroll cut-off after go-live is an end-of-month one): it deducts only its own split share of the estimate, on the view that the old system took the first half. An employee separated after the mid-month cut-off keeps the estimate's share; final pay (step 2.14) settles the rest | 2.9 | The accountant | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 228 | **Legal (RA 10963, RR 11-2018): withholding per cut-off.** BIR's semi-monthly table on the cut-off's taxable compensation (settlements from earlier cut-offs taxed in the cut-off they are paid), after the employee's SSS (MPF included), PhilHealth and Pag-IBIG shares, never below 0; no annualization before step 2.12 | 2.9 | The accountant | [Payroll computation](COMPLIANCE.md#payroll-computation-build-step-29) |
| 229 | **Legal (RR 11-2018): minimum wage earners.** The flag on the salary in effect on the pay date. Basic, holiday, overtime, premium and night differential pay are exempt; their taxable allowances and de minimis excess are still taxed on the semi-monthly table, with no contribution subtracted | 2.9 | The accountant | [Payroll computation](COMPLIANCE.md#payroll-computation-build-step-29) |
| 230 | **Legal (RR 2-98 as amended, RA 10963): de minimis excess** is taxed in the cut-off it arises, as the spec says ("taxed on the excess"), not first pooled into the ₱90,000 ceiling for 13th month pay and other benefits; step 2.12's annualization reconciles it | 2.9 | The accountant | [Payroll computation](COMPLIANCE.md#payroll-computation-build-step-29) |
| 231 | **De minimis limit periods.** A monthly limit covers both cut-offs paid in the pay date's month, a semester is January–June or July–December, a yearly limit the calendar year, a daily limit × the cut-off's present days; earlier payments count only from finalized runs; a limit set as a share of the minimum wage uses the employee's region on the pay date | 2.9 | The accountant | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 232 | **Rates: basic pay formulas.** Monthly ÷ 2 when employed the whole cut-off; a salary change inside the cut-off shares monthly ÷ 2 by base days; a hire or separation inside it pays employed base days × each day's daily rate; daily-rated pays employed base days that aren't holidays × the daily rate, holidays through holiday pay | 2.9 | The accountant | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 233 | **Base days include holidays** (the employee's scheduled work days, from the schedule in effect each day). Per cut-off allowances prorate by employed base days and aren't reduced by absences; a per day present allowance counts only the DTR's `present` days, not paid leave or paid offset | 2.9 | HR, the accountant | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 234 | **Legal (Labor Code Art. 94): the workday-before rule.** The workday before is the last scheduled work day before the holiday that isn't itself a holiday, rest days skipped. Being present (even partly), on paid leave or on paid offset meets it; an absence, leave without pay or a day before the date hired doesn't; a worked holiday is always paid. While that day's timesheet isn't approved the rule counts as met, and a later approval settles the difference | 2.9 | HR, counsel | [Payroll computation](COMPLIANCE.md#payroll-computation-build-step-29) |
| 235 | **Legal (Labor Code Art. 94) and rates: unworked special days.** Monthly-rated employees aren't deducted for one (`monthlyRatedUnworkedSpecialDay` seeded `paid`, since the 261-day factor pays every day but rest days); daily-rated employees get `specialDayUnworked` (seeded 0) | 2.9 | The accountant, counsel | [Payroll computation](COMPLIANCE.md#payroll-computation-build-step-29) |
| 236 | **Legal (DOLE holiday pay rules): an unworked regular holiday on a rest day** is paid at `regularHolidayUnworked` (100%) under the workday-before rule, for monthly- and daily-rated employees alike, as a holiday pay line | 2.9 | Counsel, the accountant | [Payroll computation](COMPLIANCE.md#payroll-computation-build-step-29) |
| 237 | **Rates: the premium formulas.** Holiday work within the first 8 hours pays the day's rate less the base already paid that day (1 for a regular holiday; for a special day 1 when a monthly salary covers it, otherwise `specialDayUnworked`); claimed minutes beyond 8 hours on a rest day or holiday pay the day's rate × (1 + 30%); ordinary overtime × (1 + 25%). Two holidays on one date count as one (300% isn't seeded) | 2.9 | The accountant | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 238 | **Legal (Labor Code Art. 86): night differential** is the DTR's night minutes × the minute rate × the day's rate × 10%, without compounding the overtime add-on for night minutes inside overtime | 2.9 | The accountant | [Payroll computation](COMPLIANCE.md#payroll-computation-build-step-29) |
| 239 | **Attendance deductions.** Absences and leave without pay at the daily rate of the day; late, undertime and partial-day absences at the minute rate (daily ÷ `hoursPerDay` ÷ 60) | 2.9 | HR, the accountant | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 240 | **Provisional pay.** A cut-off whose timesheet isn't `approved` (or has none) pays basic pay, per cut-off allowances and daily-rated holiday pay (the workday-before rule counted as met), with contributions and tax; overtime, premiums, night differential, per day present allowances, reimbursements, holiday deductions and attendance deductions wait for the approval | 2.9 | HR | [Submission & approval](modules/talent.md#submission--approval) |
| 241 | **A finalized period's basic pay and per cut-off allowances are never recomputed.** A back-dated salary or allowance change stays a manual back pay line in step 2.10, as the spec's example says, so it can never be paid twice | 2.9 | The accountant, the user | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 242 | **Settlements: the lookback and what counts as paid.** Earlier cut-offs within `adjustmentLookbackCutOffs` (seeded 24, a year) and on or after the first payroll cut-off are reconciled item by item against **finalized** runs only, so two drafts can both show a settlement until step 2.10 finalizes in cut-off order and recomputes before submit; older changes need a manual line | 2.9 | The user, the accountant | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 243 | **Legal (RA 11210): maternity leave in payroll.** Approved maternity leave is ordinary paid leave inside basic pay (full pay); there is no SSS maternity benefit line, no salary differential split, no separate tax treatment and no tracking of SSS's reimbursement. The 30-day extension without pay is leave without pay | 2.9 | The accountant, counsel | [Payroll computation](COMPLIANCE.md#payroll-computation-build-step-29) |
| 244 | **Reimbursements are part of net pay** as one non-taxable line per approved timesheet, outside every contribution basis, and kept as their own total | 2.9 | The accountant | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 245 | **Net pay below zero is kept as computed** and marked "Net pay below zero"; nothing is capped or carried forward in step 2.9, and step 2.10's review resolves it | 2.9 | HR, the accountant | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 246 | **Who is in a run.** Everyone whose employment overlaps the cut-off; one with no salary on any employed day is left out and listed ("No salary on file"); one separated before the cut-off starts is left to final pay, even with earlier cut-offs to settle | 2.9 | HR | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 247 | **The first payroll cut-off** is the first to start on or after `talent.leaveSettings.goLiveDate`, and no run can be created while it is null. HR sets the go-live date on a cut-off start; a cut-off cut by go-live is the old system's | 2.9 | HR, the user | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 248 | **Creating and refreshing drafts.** The job (05:00) creates only the latest ended cut-off's draft, once every included timesheet is approved or the submission deadline has passed, and recomputes every draft nightly; HR creates drafts for earlier ended cut-offs. Timesheet and time off approvals don't recompute drafts by themselves; HR recomputes before reviewing | 2.9 | HR, the user | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 249 | **The `PAY-YYYY-NNNN` number**, with the pay date's year, from Core's counters when the run is created | 2.9 | The user | [DATA_MODEL › Document numbers](DATA_MODEL.md#document-numbers) |
| 250 | **Legal (RA 10173): payroll run encryption and viewing.** Every amount, the run totals included, is encrypted (category `payslip`). | 251 | **Who computes.** Talent Write and HR or the System Administrator create and recompute drafts; Accounting reads only; HR computes HR staff's lines like anyone's, since computing changes no pay | 2.9 | The user | [Sensitive data](../SECURITY.md#sensitive-data) |
| 252 | **Audit granularity.** One `create` per draft and one `update` ("Recomputed") per recompute on `talent.payrollRun`, not one entry per employee line, since a draft's lines are derived values | 2.9 | The user | [Audit logging](../SECURITY.md#audit-logging) |
| 253 | **`talent.payrollDraftReady`** goes to every active HR user (the System Administrators when none is active), once per run, with no amount; a recompute notifies no one | 2.9 | HR | [Notifications](modules/talent.md#notifications) |
| 254 | **Legal (PD 851): basic salary earned** for 13th month pay is basic pay less absences, leave without pay, late, undertime, partial-day absences and holiday deductions (settlements included); it leaves out daily-rated holiday pay, overtime, premiums, night differential, allowances and reimbursements | 2.9 | The accountant | [Payroll computation](COMPLIANCE.md#payroll-computation-build-step-29) |
| 255 | **Rates: which setting version applies** follows step 2.3's dates (contributions on the first day of the contribution month; withholding, de minimis and the 13th month ceiling on the pay date; premium rates, allowance types and minimum wages on each day; `payrollSettings` and `payrollRunSettings` on the cut-off's start), and the run stores each version it used | 2.9 | The accountant | [Payroll settings and allowances](modules/talent.md#payroll-settings-and-allowances-build-step-23) |

| 257 | **Wording the spec doesn't give**: "Timesheet not approved: basic pay only. | 258 | **`talent.payrollRunSettings` beside the payroll settings, not one of them.** It is its own setting with its own view and "Add version" (`getPayrollRunSettingsView`, `addPayrollRunSettingsVersion`), like the leave settings, so the thirteen-key `PAYROLL_SETTING_KEYS` list and its page code are unchanged; the "Payroll run" card sits in the Pay schedule segment. A version may start on any day (not only a cut-off start): a run reads the version in effect on its cut-off's start, so a mid-cut-off version applies from the next cut-off | 2.9 | The user | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 259 | **`payrollRuns` also has a unique `number`**, besides the spec's unique `{ kind, periodStart }` and `{ status, periodStart }`; `settingVersions[].effectiveFrom` is null only when no version was stored (before `pnpm seed:admin`, when the seeded value applies) | 2.9 | The user | [DATA_MODEL › Collection ownership](DATA_MODEL.md#collection-ownership) |
| 260 | **The run guards.** A run changes only through an `updateOne`/`findOneAndUpdate` that filters on `status: 'draft'` and `$set`s only the recompute's fields (`computedAt`, `computedBy`, `settingVersions`, `employeeCount`, `leftOut`, `totals`) with `$inc` of `version`; it is never deleted. Lines are inserted only for a draft run (checked in the write's session) through `bulkWrite` `insertOne` operations or `create(…, { session, ordered: true })`, since `insertMany`'s middleware can't see the session and so is refused; `deleteMany({ runId })` only for a draft run's lines; every other line write is refused. Step 2.10 widens the run guard for its status moves | 2.9 | The user | [DATA_MODEL › Locked and append-only records](DATA_MODEL.md#locked-and-append-only-records) |
| 261 | **Holiday rules the spec leaves open (pay engine).** The base already paid for holiday work is what was actually paid that day: 1 when a monthly salary covers a holiday on a work day, otherwise `regularHolidayUnworked` or `specialDayUnworked` (the spec's "1 for a regular holiday" equals the seeded rate). A special day worked on a rest day also gets a holiday pay line at `specialDayUnworked` (₱0 seeded), so the day totals `specialDayOnRestDay`. With `monthlyRatedUnworkedSpecialDay` `deducted`, the deduction is a `holidayNotPaid` line "Special day not worked (<day>)" that waits for approval like the other holiday deductions. Provisional pay keeps every holiday pay line whose rule counts as met (a monthly salary's regular holiday on a rest day included, not only daily-rated); a special day worked on a rest day waits. The workday-before rule with no work day in the data is met unless the holiday is on or before the date hired, and an approved day the DTR doesn't list counts as met | 2.9 | The accountant, counsel | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 262 | **Premiums follow the extra-hours treatment, not the type's name.** The engine pays overtime and premiums from the DTR's claimed minutes by kind, which step 2.4 fills only under the `overtime` treatment of `talent.timesheetSettings.extraHours` (seeded: Overtime timesheet → overtime, Standard → offset). If HR ever maps the Standard type to `overtime`, its claimed hours are paid | 2.9 | HR, the user | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 263 | **De minimis in the engine.** Limits are counted per de minimis key (two allowance types on one key share its limit); the run service passes, per key, what finalized lines counted against it in the limit's period (their `deMinimis[].amountCentavos`). The excess is taxed on the latest lines first. A daily limit on an unapproved timesheet counts the employed base days that aren't holidays as present; a key missing from the pay date's `talent.deMinimisLimits` makes the whole amount taxable. An allowance whose type is missing from the day's `talent.allowanceTypes` version counts as a taxable per cut-off allowance | 2.9 | The accountant | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 264 | **Basic pay and bases at the edges.** Monthly ÷ 2 applies when the employee is employed every calendar day of the cut-off (hired on or before its start, not separated before its end); a base day with no salary on file earns nothing. The monthly basic salary basis falls back to the first salaried employed day of the cut-off when no salary is in effect on the first of the contribution month (or the date hired). Contribution bases below 0 (a month refunded by settlements) count as 0. The minimum wage earner flag is the salary's on the pay date; the breakdown's rate facts are the last salaried employed day's | 2.9 | The accountant | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |
| 265 | **How lines are keyed and shown.** A line of ₱0.00 is left out (a zero tax or contribution too; the breakdown keeps the figure). Holiday pay and holiday deductions are one line per holiday (`holidayPay:<date>`, `holidayNotPaid:<date>`), allowances one per type and basis (`allowance:<type>`, `allowanceDay:<type>`), the rest one per kind; settlements match items by these keys. A deduction is stored positive and a settlement signed on its own side, so "absences −₱1,000.00" is the settlement's effect on pay (`payEffectCentavos`). A settlement line shows its quantity and rate only when nothing was paid for the item before. The SSS line includes the MPF share; the breakdown keeps the split | 2.9 | The user, the accountant | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |

Its "Add version" sheet is filled in with the latest version (the seeded values when there is none), and the effective date hint says any day works but a date inside a cut-off applies from the next cut-off (258). Wording the spec doesn’t give | 2.9 | The user | [Payroll run computation](modules/talent.md#payroll-run-computation-build-step-29) |

| 269 | **What a run reads, and its window.** A run reads the earlier cut-offs within the lookback of the `talent.payrollRunSettings` version on its own cut-off's start, plus the cut-off before the oldest of them, for the workday-before rule of a holiday early in a cut-off (that cut-off's approved DTR; not approved or no timesheet counts the rule as met, decision 330). Each earlier period is computed with the `talent.payrollSettings` and run settings in effect on its own start. The holidays-changed listener recomputes a draft when a changed date falls anywhere in that window. | 319 | **The widened run guard's exact shapes** (chunk B). The status filter is `status: '<status>'` or `status: { $in: [...] }` (strings only); a recompute and the submit accept `draft`, `returned` or both. Every recompute, submit, return and finalize must `$inc` `version` by exactly 1 (step 2.9's guard let a recompute leave it out; the service always raised it). The submit's `$set` must hold `submittedAt`, `submittedBy` and `approvalRequestId`, so the service calls `submitApproval()` before the run update in the same transaction. Return's remarks are checked by the guard too (a string of 1–1,000 after trimming). Return and resubmit don't clear the earlier submit or return fields; the latest values win. Clearing `payslipsPending` takes no `$inc` and changes nothing else | 2.10 | The user | [DATA_MODEL › Collection ownership](DATA_MODEL.md#collection-ownership) |
| 320 | **The line guard's payslip writes** (chunk B). The snapshot is set by `updateOne`/`findOneAndUpdate`, or `bulkWrite` `updateOne` operations (all snapshots, no mix with inserts, no upsert), so the finalize hook can write every line in one round trip. Each is filtered on an ObjectId `runId` and `payslip: null`, sets the whole `payslip` with `fileId` and `generatedAt` null, and the run must be `pendingApproval` in the write's session. The PDF step is filtered on `runId` and `payslip.fileId: null`, sets both `payslip.fileId` and `payslip.generatedAt`, and the run must be `finalized`; the guard adds `payslip: { $type: 'object' }` to its filter so a line with no snapshot never gets a partial one | 2.10 | The user | [DATA_MODEL › Collection ownership](DATA_MODEL.md#collection-ownership) |
| 321 | **The manual entry model's own checks** (chunk B). Switches that don't apply to a kind are null (an earning has `taxable` and `countsAsBasicSalary`; a deduction has `reducesTaxablePay`, `authorizedInWriting` and `countsAsBasicSalary`; an override has only `targetLineKey`). An after-tax deduction needs `authorizedInWriting` true. A removal must supersede an entry and may leave the amount out; every other entry needs one. The amount's range (a whole number, 1 or more, or 0 or more for an override) and `targetLineKey` being one of the engine's overridable lines are checked by the service, since the model only sees ciphertext and the key list is the engine's | 2.10 | The user | [DATA_MODEL › Collection ownership](DATA_MODEL.md#collection-ownership) |
| 322 | **One acknowledgement per run, employee and flag** is a unique index (`onePayrollFlagAcknowledgement`), reading the spec's "one per run, employee and flag"; DATA_MODEL lists the index without saying unique | 2.10 | The user | [DATA_MODEL › Collection ownership](DATA_MODEL.md#collection-ownership) |
| 323 | **The payslip snapshot's shape** (chunk B). `employeeName` and `employeeNumber` are required; `departmentName` and `positionTitle` may be null; `bank` is `{ bank (unionbank or rcbc), accountName (may be null), accountNumber (encrypted) }` or null; `ytd` is one encrypted JSON string whose keys the finalize service decides. Old step 2.9 lines read as `flags` [], `manualEntryCount` 0 and `payslip` null; runs read `flagCounts` as all 0 | 2.10 | The user | [DATA_MODEL › Collection ownership](DATA_MODEL.md#collection-ownership) |
| 324 | **Manual lines in the engine** (chunk A). A manual earning or deduction is a line of kind `manual` (key `manual:<entry id>`) dated the run's own cut-off: earnings after the computed earnings, deductions after the contributions and tax; its label is printed as typed, with "Adjustment: " in front on the payslip. An override keeps the computed line in its place with the new amount and `override.computedAmountCentavos`. Manual lines are never attendance items, so a later run never settles them | 2.10 | The user | [Payroll review, approval and payslips](modules/talent.md#payroll-review-approval-and-payslips-build-step-210) |
| 325 | **Legal (RA 10963, RR 11-2018): a minimum wage earner's manual entries** (chunk A). A taxable manual earning, and a deduction that reduces taxable pay, that does **not** count as basic salary is taxed for a minimum wage earner like a taxable allowance (net of such deductions, never below 0); one that counts as basic salary (back pay of the minimum wage) stays exempt with the rest of the minimum wage. Both still count in the compensation for contributions. *Assumption, to be confirmed by the accountant* | 2.10 | The accountant, counsel | [Payroll approval and payslips](COMPLIANCE.md#payroll-approval-and-payslips-build-step-210) |
| 326 | **How an override is worked through** (chunk A). Basic pay or a taxable per cut-off allowance stays taxable at the overridden amount; a de minimis allowance's excess is worked out again from it; a non-taxable allowance stays non-taxable. A contribution or EC override replaces that agency's share for **this cut-off** (the month's own figures stay as computed), so the same month's end-of-month run reads it as what the mid-month run deducted and deducts the rest of the month. A withholding tax override replaces the tax (and year-to-date tax), while the taxable compensation shown stays as computed. *Assumption, to be confirmed by the accountant* | 2.10 | The accountant | [Payroll review, approval and payslips](modules/talent.md#payroll-review-approval-and-payslips-build-step-210) |
| 327 | **Override targets and stale entries** (chunk A). The contribution, EC and tax lines are always override targets, even at ₱0.00 (an overridden line is kept and shown at ₱0.00; one not overridden at ₱0.00 is still left out). Basic pay and a per cut-off allowance must be on the computed line. An entry the engine can't apply is kept in the breakdown's `manualEntries` with a reason: `notOverridable` (an attendance item or unknown key, also refused by the form), `targetMissing` (its line isn't computed now), `duplicateTarget` (a later live override of the same line wins); the service adds `employeeNotInRun`. Each makes the line's `manualEntryStale` flag | 2.10 | The user | [Payroll review, approval and payslips](modules/talent.md#payroll-review-approval-and-payslips-build-step-210) |
| 328 | **Breakdown version 2** (chunk A). Every compute writes `breakdownVersion` 2 (each line's `manual` and `override`, and the line's `manualEntries`); a stored version 1 is read with those null and empty and its version kept as 1, and written again as version 2. Step 2.9's test that refused a "version 2" breakdown now refuses version 3 | 2.10 | The user | [DATA_MODEL › Collection ownership](DATA_MODEL.md#collection-ownership) |
| 329 | **The flags' `items`** (chunk A). `onboardingIncomplete` lists each missing item as `<item>:<state>` (`bankAccount:waitingForVerification` reads "payroll bank account not verified", `governmentIds:todo` "government IDs not entered"); `belowMinimumWage` lists the days below; `manualEntryStale` the entry ids; the others none. `timesheetNotApproved` is set whenever the timesheet isn't approved, no timesheet included. A day with no minimum wage on file is never below | 2.10 | HR | [Payroll review, approval and payslips](modules/talent.md#payroll-review-approval-and-payslips-build-step-210) |
| 330 | **Year-to-date's figures** (chunk A). A line's own figures are its gross pay (reimbursements and settled earnings included, as the run's gross is), its tax withheld and its employee shares for the cut-off, each after overrides. The sum takes the opening entry of the pay date's year entered last, plus the given lines paid in that year whose cut-off starts on or before this run's. The frozen `payslip.ytd` is one JSON string with `ytdVersion` 1 | 2.10 | The accountant | [Payroll review, approval and payslips](modules/talent.md#payroll-review-approval-and-payslips-build-step-210) |
| 331 | **The payslip's lines** (chunk A). Each printed line is its label and amount only: no quantity, rate or computation fact. Settlements print under "Adjustments from earlier cut-offs" signed by their effect on net pay (an absence reversed reads −₱1,000.00 as a deduction settled back, an overtime paid late +₱1,250.00). The account shows its last 4 digits only, digits counted after removing separators | 2.10 | HR, the user | [Payroll review, approval and payslips](modules/talent.md#payroll-review-approval-and-payslips-build-step-210) |
| 332 | **The manual entry form** (chunk A). Label 1–80 and reason 1–500, both trimmed; an earning or deduction ₱0.01 or more, an override ₱0.00 or more; an override has no label of its own (the service gives it its line's label). The after-tax deduction refusal reads "Tick “authorized in writing by the employee or required by law” for a deduction after tax." | 2.10 | The user | [Payroll review, approval and payslips](modules/talent.md#payroll-review-approval-and-payslips-build-step-210) |
| 333 | **The status label** `pendingApproval` reads "Waiting for Board approval" everywhere (it read "Waiting for approval" in step 2.9, which never wrote it), as the spec's screens say | 2.10 | The user | [Payroll review, approval and payslips](modules/talent.md#payroll-review-approval-and-payslips-build-step-210) |
| 334 | **The payslip PDF has no company TIN** (chunk P). It prints the logo, registered name and address, as talent.md's Payslips list says; the TIN isn't on that list.
